              paymentStatus: isConfirmed ? "confirmed" : isFailed || isCancelled ? "pending" : (o.payment_status as any) || "pending",
              status: isFailed ? "error" : isCancelled ? "cancelled" : isPrinting ? "printing" : isQueued ? "queued" : isDone ? "done" : "error",
              detail: isFailed ? "Print failed on spooler" : isCancelled ? "Cancelled" : `Token ${o.token_code || ""}`,
              timestamp: new Date(o.created_at),
            });
          });
          prev.forEach((p) => {
            const existing = map.get(p.sessionId);
            if (existing && (existing.status === "error" || existing.status === "cancelled")) {
              map.set(p.sessionId, {
                ...p,
                ...existing,
                tokenCode: p.tokenCode || existing?.tokenCode,
                totalAmount:
                  p.totalAmount !== undefined && p.totalAmount > 0
                    ? p.totalAmount
                    : existing?.totalAmount,
              });
            } else {
              map.set(p.sessionId, {
                ...existing,
                ...p,
                tokenCode: p.tokenCode || existing?.tokenCode,
                totalAmount:
                  p.totalAmount !== undefined && p.totalAmount > 0
                    ? p.totalAmount
                    : existing?.totalAmount,
              });
            }
          });
          return Array.from(map.values());
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[App] Could not load today's count:", e);
    }
  }, []);

  // Midnight day rollover watcher: automatically resets token counter every day
  useEffect(() => {
    let lastDate = new Date().toDateString();
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const currentDate = new Date().toDateString();
      if (currentDate !== lastDate) {
        lastDate = currentDate;
        console.log("[App] Calendar day rolled over, resetting today's token counter");
        setTodayPrintsCount(0);
        if (shopId) {
          loadTodayPrintsCount(shopId);
        }
      }
    }, 60000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [shopId, loadTodayPrintsCount]);

  // 3. Load printer assignments into ref for automatic routing
  const refreshAssignments = useCallback((id: string) => {
    try {
      const cached = localStorage.getItem(`printap_printers_${id}`);
      if (cached) {
        assignmentsRef.current = JSON.parse(cached);
        return;
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }

    // Auto-discover and register default Windows printer if assignments are empty
    if (Object.keys(assignmentsRef.current).length === 0 && window.electronAPI?.listPrinters) {
      window.electronAPI.listPrinters().then((res) => {
        if (res?.success && res.printers && res.printers.length > 0) {
          const physicalPrinters = res.printers.filter((p) => p.isPhysical);
          if (physicalPrinters.length === 0) return;
          const defaultP = physicalPrinters.find((p) => p.isDefault) || physicalPrinters[0];
          const autoMap: Record<string, PrinterAssignment> = {
            [defaultP.name]: {
              shop_id: id,
              printer_name: defaultP.name,
              printer_number: 1,
              role: "both",
              is_default: true,
              is_active: true,
            },
          };
          assignmentsRef.current = autoMap;
          try {
            localStorage.setItem(`printap_printers_${id}`, JSON.stringify(autoMap));
          } catch {}
          console.log(`[App] Auto-assigned default physical printer "${defaultP.name}" as Printer #1`);
        }
      }).catch((err) => console.warn("[App] Failed to auto-assign default printer:", err));
    }
  }, []);

  // Wire FIFO Print Dispatcher callbacks
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!fifoDispatcherRef.current) {
      fifoDispatcherRef.current = new FifoPrintDispatcher(
        shopId || "default",
        todayPrintsCount
      );
    } else if (shopId) {
      fifoDispatcherRef.current.setShopId(shopId, todayPrintsCount);
    }

    fifoDispatcherRef.current.setCallbacks({
      onJobStarted: (job, printerName) => {
        console.log(
          `[App] Dispatcher: Job ${job.tokenCode} started printing on ${printerName}`
        );
        const isAlreadyConfirmed = confirmedPaymentSessionsRef.current.has(
          job.sessionId
        );

        setActiveJob((prev) => ({
          sessionId: job.sessionId,
          tokenCode: job.tokenCode,
          fileCount: job.files.length,
          files: job.files.map((f) => ({
            fileName: f.fileName,
            fileSize: f.fileSize,
            pageCount: f.pageCount,
            settings: f.settings,
          })),
          totalAmount: job.totalAmount,
          paymentMethod: job.paymentMethod || prev?.paymentMethod || "upi",
          paymentStatus:
            isAlreadyConfirmed || (prev?.sessionId === job.sessionId && prev?.paymentStatus === "confirmed")
              ? "confirmed"
              : (job.paymentStatus as any) || "pending",
          fileName:
            job.files.length > 1
              ? `${job.files.length} documents (${job.tokenCode})`
              : job.files[0]?.fileName,
          status: "printing",
          detail: `Token ${job.tokenCode} (${job.files.length} file(s)) spooling to ${printerName}...`,
          timestamp: new Date(),
        }));
      },

      onJobFileProgress: (
        sessionId,
        fileName,
        fileIndex,
        totalFiles,
        printerName
      ) => {
        setActiveJob((prev) => {
          if (!prev || prev.sessionId !== sessionId) return prev;
          return {
            ...prev,
            status: "printing",
            detail: `Printing ${fileIndex}/${totalFiles}: ${fileName} on ${printerName}`,
          };
        });
      },

      onJobCompleted: async (job, printerName) => {
        console.log(
          `[App] Dispatcher: Job ${job.tokenCode} completed on ${printerName}`
        );
        const isConfirmed = confirmedPaymentSessionsRef.current.has(job.sessionId);
        const paymentConfirmed = isConfirmed || job.paymentStatus === "confirmed";

        if (paymentConfirmed) {
          addBoundedSet(confirmedPaymentSessionsRef.current, job.sessionId);
        }

        // Record print event in Supabase ledger with backoff retry
        let insertSuccess = false;
        for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 3; attempt++) {
          try {
            const supabase = getSupabase();
            const { error } = await supabase.from("print_events").insert({
              shop_id: shopId,
              session_id: job.sessionId,
            });
            if (error) throw error;
            insertSuccess = true;
            break;
          } catch (err) {
            console.warn(`[App] Could not insert print_event (attempt ${attempt}/3):`, err);
            if (attempt < 3) {
              await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 1000 * attempt));
            }
          }
        }
        if (!insertSuccess) {
          console.error(`[App] CRITICAL: Failed to record print_event for session ${job.sessionId} after 3 attempts`);
        }

        const completedJob: LiveJob = {
          sessionId: job.sessionId,
          tokenCode: job.tokenCode,
          fileCount: job.files.length,
          files: job.files.map((f) => ({
            fileName: f.fileName,
            fileSize: f.fileSize,
            pageCount: f.pageCount,
            settings: f.settings,
          })),
          totalAmount: job.totalAmount,
          paymentMethod: job.paymentMethod || "upi",
          paymentStatus: paymentConfirmed ? "confirmed" : (job.paymentStatus as any) || "pending",
          paymentConfirmedAt: paymentConfirmed ? new Date() : undefined,
          fileName:
            job.files.length > 1
              ? `${job.files.length} documents`
              : job.files[0]?.fileName,
          status: "done",
          detail: `Token ${job.tokenCode} — ${job.files.length} file(s) printed & shredded`,
          timestamp: new Date(),
        };

        setRecentJobs((prev) => {
          const filtered = prev.filter((j) => j.sessionId !== job.sessionId);
          return [completedJob, ...filtered.slice(0, 19)];
        });
        setTodayPrintsCount((c) => c + job.files.length);

        setActiveJob((prev) => {
          if (!prev || prev.sessionId !== job.sessionId) return prev;
          return {
            ...prev,
            tokenCode: job.tokenCode,
            totalAmount: job.totalAmount,
            paymentStatus: paymentConfirmed ? "confirmed" : prev.paymentStatus,
            status: "done",
            detail: `Order ${job.tokenCode} complete! Paper ready on tray.`,
          };
        });

        setTimeout(() => {
          setActiveJob((prev) => (prev?.sessionId === job.sessionId ? null : prev));
        }, 5000);
      },

      onJobFailed: async (job, error, printerName) => {
        console.error(
          `[App] Dispatcher: Job ${job.tokenCode} failed on ${printerName}:`,
          error
        );

        // Update database print_orders status to failed
        try {
          const supabase = getSupabase();
          await supabase
            .from("print_orders")
            .update({ status: "failed" })
            .eq("session_id", job.sessionId);
        } catch (dbErr) {
          console.warn("[App] Could not update print_order status to failed:", dbErr);
        }

        // NOTE: We do not broadcast error to customer immediately here.
        // We allow the shopkeeper to inspect the printer, fix paper/offline, and hit "Retry".
        // The customer error modal will only be triggered if the shopkeeper explicitly closes/dismisses or cancels.

        const failedJob: LiveJob = {
          sessionId: job.sessionId,
          tokenCode: job.tokenCode,
          fileCount: job.files.length,
          files: job.files.map((f) => ({
            fileName: f.fileName,
            fileSize: f.fileSize,
            pageCount: f.pageCount,
            settings: f.settings,
          })),
          fileName: job.files[0]?.fileName || `Print #${job.sessionId.slice(0, 8)}`,
          status: "error",
          detail: `${error} (${printerName})`,
          timestamp: new Date(),
          totalAmount: job.totalAmount,
          paymentMethod: job.paymentMethod,
          paymentStatus: "pending",
        };
        setRecentJobs((prev) => {
          const filtered = prev.filter((j) => j.sessionId !== job.sessionId);
          return [failedJob, ...filtered.slice(0, 19)];
        });
        setActiveJob(failedJob);
      },
    });
  }, [shopId]);

  // 4. Listen to hardware status events from Electron Main
  useEffect(() => {
    const cleanup = window.electronAPI.onPrintStatus((data) => {
      setActiveJob((prev) => {
        if (!prev || prev.sessionId !== data.sessionId) return prev;
        let newStatus = prev.status;
        if (data.stage === "saving" || data.stage === "saved") newStatus = "saving";
        else if (data.stage === "printing" || data.stage === "printed")
          newStatus = "printing";
        else if (data.stage === "shredding" || data.stage === "shredded")
          newStatus = "shredding";
        else if (data.stage === "complete") newStatus = "done";
        else if (data.stage === "error") newStatus = "error";

        return {
          ...prev,
          status: newStatus,
          stage: data.stage,
          detail: data.detail,
        };
      });
    });

    return cleanup;
  }, []);

  const handleToggleOnline = useCallback(
    async (online: boolean) => {
      if (!shopId) return;

      setIsTogglingOnline(true);
      try {
        if (online) {
          try {
            const result = await window.electronAPI.listPrinters();
            const physicalPrinters = (result?.printers || []).filter((p: any) => p.isPhysical);
            if (physicalPrinters.length === 0) {
              setToastMessage("Cannot go online: No physical printer connected. Please connect a printer and try again.");
              return;
            }
          } catch (err) {
            console.error("Failed to check printers:", err);
            setToastMessage("Failed to check connected printers.");
            return;
          }

          const res = await startPresence(shopId);
          if (res.success) {
            setIsShopOnline(true);
            try {
              localStorage.setItem("printap_shop_online_pref", "true");
            } catch {}
          }
        } else {
          await stopPresence();
          setIsShopOnline(false);
          try {
            localStorage.setItem("printap_shop_online_pref", "false");
          } catch {}
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("[App] Toggle online error:", err);
      } finally {
        setIsTogglingOnline(false);
      }
    },
    [shopId]
  );

  // Synchronize presence on authentication or shop change
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAuthenticated || !shopId) {
      stopPresence();
      return;
    }

    let isMounted = true;
    const initPresence = async () => {
      // Default to ONLINE automatically upon login/launch so customers can connect immediately.
      // If the shop owner previously explicitly chose to go offline, honor that preference.
      const pref = localStorage.getItem("printap_shop_online_pref");
      const shouldBeOnline = pref !== "false";

      if (shouldBeOnline) {
        try {
          const result = await window.electronAPI.listPrinters();
          const physicalPrinters = (result?.printers || []).filter((p: any) => p.isPhysical);
          if (physicalPrinters.length === 0) {
            console.warn("[App] No physical printers found, staying offline.");
            await setShopOnline(shopId, false);
            if (isMounted) setIsShopOnline(false);
            return;
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("[App] Failed to check printers during initPresence:", err);
        }

        console.log(`[App] Automatically setting terminal online for shop ${shopId}...`);
        const res = await startPresence(shopId);
        if (isMounted && res.success) {
          setIsShopOnline(true);
        }
      } else {
        await setShopOnline(shopId, false);
        if (isMounted) {
          setIsShopOnline(false);
        }
      }
    };

    initPresence();

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [isAuthenticated, shopId]);

  // Graceful shutdown listener from Electron Main
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!window.electronAPI?.onAppWillQuit) return;
    const cleanup = window.electronAPI.onAppWillQuit(async () => {
      console.log("[App] onAppWillQuit received, stopping presence...");
      await stopPresence();
      if (window.electronAPI.confirmQuit) {
        window.electronAPI.confirmQuit();
      }
    });
    return cleanup;
  }, []);

  // 5. Connect WebRTC Signaling when authenticated AND shop is online
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAuthenticated || !shopId || !isShopOnline) {
      setIsListening(false);
      return;
    }

    loadTodayPrintsCount(shopId);
    refreshAssignments(shopId);

    console.log(`[App] Initializing signaling for shop ${shopId}...`);
    setIsListening(true);

    const channel = startShopSignaling(shopId, {
      onSessionStart: (sessionId) => {
        setActiveJob((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          sessionId,
          status: "receiving",
          progressPct: 0,
          timestamp: prev?.timestamp || new Date(),
        }));
      },

      onRequestToken: async (sessionId, hasColor, totalAmount, fileCount) => {
        refreshAssignments(shopId);
        if (!fifoDispatcherRef.current) {
          fifoDispatcherRef.current = new FifoPrintDispatcher(shopId, todayPrintsCount);
        }

        const allocation = fifoDispatcherRef.current.allocateToken(
          sessionId,
          hasColor,
          assignmentsRef.current
        );

        console.log(
          `[App] Atomically allocated local token ${allocation.tokenCode} (seq #${allocation.tokenCode}) for session ${sessionId.slice(0, 8)}`
        );

        // Broadcast assign-token and order-status back to customer immediately
        if (signalingChannelRef.current) {
          try {
            await broadcastTokenAssigned(signalingChannelRef.current, {
              sessionId,
              tokenCode: allocation.tokenCode,
              printerNumber: allocation.printerNumber,
              queuePosition: allocation.queuePosition,
            });
            await broadcastOrderStatus(signalingChannelRef.current, {
              sessionId,
              tokenCode: allocation.tokenCode,
              printerNumber: allocation.printerNumber,
              queuePosition: allocation.queuePosition,
              paymentStatus: confirmedPaymentSessionsRef.current.has(sessionId)
                ? "confirmed"
                : "pending",
            });
          } catch (err) {
            console.warn("[App] Error broadcasting assign-token:", err);
          }
        }

        const isConfirmed = confirmedPaymentSessionsRef.current.has(sessionId);
        setActiveJob((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          sessionId,
          tokenCode: allocation.tokenCode,
          totalAmount: totalAmount || prev?.totalAmount || 0,
          paymentMethod: prev?.paymentMethod || "upi",
          paymentStatus: isConfirmed ? "confirmed" : "pending",
          fileCount: fileCount || prev?.fileCount || 1,
          status: "receiving",
          detail: `Assigned Token ${allocation.tokenCode} — ready for document stream`,
          timestamp: new Date(),
        }));
      },

      onManifestReceived: async (sessionId, manifestOrder) => {
        addBoundedSet(handledOrderSessions.current, sessionId);
        refreshAssignments(shopId);
        if (!fifoDispatcherRef.current) {
          fifoDispatcherRef.current = new FifoPrintDispatcher(shopId, todayPrintsCount);
        }

        const hasColor =
          manifestOrder.finishingServices?.some((f: any) => f.colorMode === "color") || false;

        // Use already allocated token or atomically allocate one locally
        const allocation =
          fifoDispatcherRef.current.getAllocatedToken(sessionId) ||
          fifoDispatcherRef.current.allocateToken(sessionId, hasColor, assignmentsRef.current);

        const token = allocation.tokenCode;
        const amount = manifestOrder.totalAmount;
        const isAlreadyConfirmed = confirmedPaymentSessionsRef.current.has(sessionId);

        // Broadcast authoritative token status back to customer
        if (signalingChannelRef.current) {
          try {
            await broadcastOrderStatus(signalingChannelRef.current, {
              sessionId,
              tokenCode: token,
              printerNumber: allocation.printerNumber,
              queuePosition: allocation.queuePosition,
              paymentStatus: isAlreadyConfirmed
                ? "confirmed"
                : manifestOrder.paymentStatus || "pending",
            });
          } catch (err) {
            console.warn("[App] Error broadcasting initial order status:", err);
          }
        }

        setActiveJob((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          sessionId,
          tokenCode: token,
          totalAmount: amount !== undefined && amount > 0 ? amount : prev?.totalAmount,
          paymentMethod: manifestOrder.paymentMethod || prev?.paymentMethod || "upi",
          paymentStatus:
            isAlreadyConfirmed || (prev?.sessionId === sessionId && prev?.paymentStatus === "confirmed")
              ? "confirmed"
              : (manifestOrder.paymentStatus as any) || prev?.paymentStatus || "pending",
          fileCount: manifestOrder.totalFiles,
          status: "receiving",
          detail: `Receiving order ${token} (${manifestOrder.totalFiles} files)...`,
          timestamp: prev?.timestamp || new Date(),
        }));

        setRecentJobs((prev) => {
          const existing = prev.find((j) => j.sessionId === sessionId);
          const paymentConfirmed =
            isAlreadyConfirmed ||
            existing?.paymentStatus === "confirmed" ||
            manifestOrder.paymentStatus === "confirmed";

          if (paymentConfirmed) {
            addBoundedSet(confirmedPaymentSessionsRef.current, sessionId);
          }

          const filtered = prev.filter((j) => j.sessionId !== sessionId);
          const initialJob: LiveJob = {
            sessionId,
            tokenCode: token,
            totalAmount: amount !== undefined && amount > 0 ? amount : existing?.totalAmount,
            paymentMethod: manifestOrder.paymentMethod || existing?.paymentMethod || "upi",
            paymentStatus: paymentConfirmed ? "confirmed" : existing?.paymentStatus || "pending",
            paymentConfirmedAt: existing?.paymentConfirmedAt,
            fileCount: manifestOrder.totalFiles,
            fileName: `Order ${token}`,
            status: "receiving",
            detail: `Receiving ${manifestOrder.totalFiles} file(s)...`,
            timestamp: existing?.timestamp || new Date(),
          };
          return [initialJob, ...filtered.slice(0, 19)];
        });
      },

      onFileProgress: (sessionId, fileName, pct, _received, _total, fileIndex, totalFiles) => {
        setActiveJob((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          sessionId,
          fileName:
            totalFiles && totalFiles > 1
              ? `File ${fileIndex || 1}/${totalFiles}: ${fileName}`
              : fileName,
          status: "receiving",
          progressPct: pct,
          fileCount: totalFiles,
          timestamp: prev?.timestamp || new Date(),
        }));
      },

      onOrderReceived: async (receivedOrder) => {
        const { sessionId, order, files } = receivedOrder;
        addBoundedSet(handledOrderSessions.current, sessionId);

        refreshAssignments(shopId);
        if (!fifoDispatcherRef.current) {
          fifoDispatcherRef.current = new FifoPrintDispatcher(shopId, todayPrintsCount);
        }

        const hasColor = files.some((f) => f.settings?.colorMode === "color");
        const allocation =
          fifoDispatcherRef.current.getAllocatedToken(sessionId) ||
          fifoDispatcherRef.current.allocateToken(sessionId, hasColor, assignmentsRef.current);

        const finalTokenCode = allocation.tokenCode;
        const finalAmount =
          order.totalAmount !== undefined && order.totalAmount > 0
            ? order.totalAmount
            : activeJob?.totalAmount !== undefined && activeJob.totalAmount > 0
            ? activeJob.totalAmount
            : 0;

        const isAlreadyConfirmed = confirmedPaymentSessionsRef.current.has(sessionId);

        console.log(
          `[App] Order received: Token ${finalTokenCode} (₹${finalAmount}) for printer #${allocation.printerNumber}, ${files.length} files. Enqueueing into FIFO queue.`
        );

        // Update Supabase print_orders token_code asynchronously
        try {
          const supabase = getSupabase();
          await supabase
            .from("print_orders")
            .update({ token_code: finalTokenCode })
            .eq("session_id", sessionId);
        } catch {}

        // Instead of enqueuing directly, add it to pendingQueue for shop owner to confirm.
        const newJob: QueuedPrintJob = {
          sessionId,
          tokenCode: finalTokenCode,
          printerNumber: allocation.printerNumber,
          targetPrinterName: allocation.targetPrinterName,
          hasColor,
          files: files.map((f) => ({
            fileId: f.fileId,
            fileName: f.fileName,
            fileSize: f.fileSize,
            pageCount: f.pageCount,
            settings: f.settings,
            buffer: f.buffer,
          })),
          totalAmount: finalAmount,
          paymentMethod: order.paymentMethod || "upi",
          paymentStatus: isAlreadyConfirmed ? "confirmed" : (order.paymentStatus as any) || "pending",
          enqueuedAt: new Date(),
        };

        setPendingQueue(prev => {
          const filtered = prev.filter(j => j.sessionId !== sessionId);
          return [...filtered, newJob];
        });

        // Clear active streaming job now that files have arrived into the queue
        setActiveJob((prev) => (prev?.sessionId === sessionId ? null : prev));

        setRecentJobs((prev) =>
          prev.map((j) =>
            j.sessionId === sessionId
              ? {
                  ...j,
                  status: "queued",
                  detail: `Files received (${files.length}) — awaiting print`,
                  progressPct: 100,
                  fileCount: files.length,
                  files: files.map((f) => ({
                    fileName: f.fileName,
                    fileSize: f.fileSize,
                    pageCount: f.pageCount,
                    settings: f.settings,
                  })),
                }
              : j
          )
        );

        // Tell the customer we received the files and are waiting for confirmation
        if (signalingChannelRef.current) {
          try {
             broadcastOrderStatus(signalingChannelRef.current, {
               sessionId,
               tokenCode: finalTokenCode,
               status: "pending_payment", 
             });
          } catch {}
        }
      },

      onFileReceived: async (sessionId, buffer, fileName) => {
        // If this session was already handled as a multi-file order, skip
        if (handledOrderSessions.current.has(sessionId)) {
          return;
        }

        refreshAssignments(shopId);
        if (!fifoDispatcherRef.current) {
          fifoDispatcherRef.current = new FifoPrintDispatcher(shopId, todayPrintsCount);
        }

        const allocation =
          fifoDispatcherRef.current.getAllocatedToken(sessionId) ||
          fifoDispatcherRef.current.allocateToken(sessionId, false, assignmentsRef.current);

        const isLegacyConfirmed = confirmedPaymentSessionsRef.current.has(sessionId);

        console.log(
          `[App] Legacy file received: ${fileName || sessionId}. Enqueueing into FIFO queue with Token ${allocation.tokenCode}`
        );

        fifoDispatcherRef.current.enqueue(
          {
            sessionId,
            tokenCode: allocation.tokenCode,
            printerNumber: allocation.printerNumber,
            targetPrinterName: allocation.targetPrinterName,
            hasColor: false,
            files: [
              {
                fileName: fileName || "Document.pdf",
                fileSize: buffer.byteLength,
                pageCount: 1,
                settings: {
                  copies: 1,
                  colorMode: "bw",
                  duplex: "single",
                  pagesPerSheet: 1,
                  pageFit: "fit",
                  orientation: "portrait",
                },
                buffer,
              },
            ],
            totalAmount: 0,
            paymentMethod: "cash",
            paymentStatus: isLegacyConfirmed ? "confirmed" : "pending",
            enqueuedAt: new Date(),
          },
          assignmentsRef.current
        );
      },

      onSessionError: (sessionId, error) => {
        console.error(`[App] Session ${sessionId} error:`, error);
        setActiveJob((prev) =>
          prev && prev.sessionId === sessionId ? null : prev
        );
        setPendingQueue((prev) => {
          const target = prev.find((j) => j.sessionId === sessionId);
          if (target) {
            target.files.forEach((f) => {
              (f as { buffer: ArrayBuffer | null }).buffer = new ArrayBuffer(0);
            });
          }
          return prev.filter((j) => j.sessionId !== sessionId);
        });
        setRecentJobs((prev) =>
          prev.map((j) =>
            j.sessionId === sessionId
              ? { ...j, status: "error", detail: error }
              : j
          )
        );
        try {
          const supabase = getSupabase();
          supabase
            .from("print_orders")
            .update({ status: "failed" })
            .eq("session_id", sessionId)
            .then(() => {});
        } catch {}
      },
    });

    signalingChannelRef.current = channel;

    return () => {
      console.log(`[App] Tearing down signaling channel...`);
      signalingChannelRef.current = null;
      stopShopSignaling(channel);
      setIsListening(false);
    };
  }, [isAuthenticated, shopId, isShopOnline, loadTodayPrintsCount, refreshAssignments]);

  // Auto-expire unpaid pendingQueue jobs older than 5 minutes to free V8 ArrayBuffer memory
  useEffect(() => {
    const EXPIRY_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      const now = Date.now();
      setPendingQueue((prev) => {
        const expired = prev.filter(
          (job) => now - new Date(job.enqueuedAt).getTime() > EXPIRY_MS
        );
        if (expired.length === 0) return prev;

        expired.forEach((job) => {
          console.log(`[App] Auto-expiring abandoned unpaid order ${job.sessionId}`);
          job.files.forEach((f) => {
            (f as { buffer: ArrayBuffer | null }).buffer = new ArrayBuffer(0);
          });
          const supabase = getSupabase();
          supabase
            .from("print_orders")
            .update({ status: "cancelled", updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
            .eq("session_id", job.sessionId)
            .then(() => {});
        });

        return prev.filter(
          (job) => now - new Date(job.enqueuedAt).getTime() <= EXPIRY_MS
        );
      });
    }, 60_000);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [shopId]);

  // Payment Confirmation Handler called from LiveDashboard (StrictMode-safe)
  const handleConfirmPayment = useCallback(
    async (sessionId: string) => {
      console.log(`[App] Confirming payment and enqueuing for session ${sessionId}...`);
      addBoundedSet(confirmedPaymentSessionsRef.current, sessionId);
      fifoDispatcherRef.current?.updateJobPaymentStatus(
        sessionId,
        "confirmed",
        assignmentsRef.current
      );

      let matchedJob: QueuedPrintJob | undefined;
      setPendingQueue((prev) => {
        const idx = prev.findIndex((j) => j.sessionId === sessionId);
        if (idx === -1) return prev;
        matchedJob = { ...prev[idx], paymentStatus: "confirmed" };
        return prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx);
      });

      if (matchedJob) {
        fifoDispatcherRef.current?.enqueue(matchedJob, assignmentsRef.current);
      }

      setActiveJob((prev) => {
        if (!prev || prev.sessionId !== sessionId) return prev;
        return {
          ...prev,
          paymentStatus: "confirmed",
          paymentConfirmedAt: new Date(),
        };
      });

      setRecentJobs((prev) =>
        prev.map((job) =>
          job.sessionId === sessionId
            ? {
                ...job,
                paymentStatus: "confirmed",
                paymentConfirmedAt: new Date(),
              }
            : job
        )
      );

      const targetJob =
        (activeJobRef.current?.sessionId === sessionId ? activeJobRef.current : null) ||
        recentJobsRef.current.find((j) => j.sessionId === sessionId);

      if (signalingChannelRef.current) {
        try {
          await broadcastPaymentConfirmed(
            signalingChannelRef.current,
            sessionId,
            targetJob?.tokenCode
          );
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn("[App] Error broadcasting payment confirmation:", err);
        }
      }

      try {
        const supabase = getSupabase();
        await supabase
          .from("print_orders")
          .update({
            payment_status: "confirmed",
            status: "queued",
            updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          })
          .eq("session_id", sessionId);
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("[App] Error updating print_orders in Supabase:", err);
      }
    },
    []
  );

  // Cancel Order Handler (Releases ArrayBuffers & Refunds Escrowed Shop Token)
  const handleCancelOrder = useCallback(
    async (sessionId: string) => {
      console.log(`[App] Cancelling order for session ${sessionId}...`);

      setPendingQueue((prev) => {
        const target = prev.find((j) => j.sessionId === sessionId);
        if (target) {
          target.files.forEach((f) => {
            (f as { buffer: ArrayBuffer | null }).buffer = new ArrayBuffer(0);
          });
        }
        return prev.filter((j) => j.sessionId !== sessionId);
      });

      fifoDispatcherRef.current?.cancelJob(sessionId, assignmentsRef.current);
      setActiveJob((prev) => (prev?.sessionId === sessionId ? null : prev));
      setRecentJobs((prev) =>
        prev.map((job) =>
          job.sessionId === sessionId ? { ...job, status: "cancelled" } : job
        )
      );

      if (signalingChannelRef.current) {
        try {
          await broadcastOrderStatus(signalingChannelRef.current, {
            sessionId,
            status: "cancelled",
          });
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn("[App] Error broadcasting cancel status to customer:", err);
        }
      }

      try {
        const supabase = getSupabase();
        await supabase
          .from("print_orders")
          .update({
            status: "cancelled",
            updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          })
          .eq("session_id", sessionId);

      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("[App] Error cancelling print_orders:", err);
      }
    },
    [shopId]
  );

  // Retry Failed Print Job Handler called from LiveDashboard
  const handleRetryJob = useCallback(
    (sessionId: string) => {
      if (!fifoDispatcherRef.current || !shopId) return;
      refreshAssignments(shopId);
      const success = fifoDispatcherRef.current.retryJob(sessionId, assignmentsRef.current);
      if (success) {
        console.log(`[App] Retrying failed print job: ${sessionId}`);
        setActiveJob((prev) => {
          if (prev && prev.sessionId === sessionId) {
            return {
              ...prev,
              status: "printing",
              detail: "Retrying print job on hardware spooler...",
            };
          }
          const jobInRecent = recentJobsRef.current.find((j) => j.sessionId === sessionId);
          if (jobInRecent) {
            return {
              ...jobInRecent,
              status: "printing",
              detail: "Retrying print job on hardware spooler...",
            };
          }
          return prev;
        });

        setRecentJobs((prev) =>
          prev.map((j) =>
            j.sessionId === sessionId
              ? {
                  ...j,
                  status: "printing",
                  detail: "Retrying print job on hardware spooler...",
                }
              : j
          )
        );
      }
    },
    [shopId, refreshAssignments]
  );

  // Dismiss Failed Job Handler
  const handleDismissJob = useCallback(
    (sessionId: string) => {
      fifoDispatcherRef.current?.dismissFailedJob(sessionId);
      setActiveJob((prev) => (prev?.sessionId === sessionId ? null : prev));
      setRecentJobs((prev) =>
        prev.map((j) =>
          j.sessionId === sessionId
            ? { ...j, status: "error", detail: "Dismissed by counter operator" }
            : j
        )
      );

      // Explicitly notify customer side now that the shop has dismissed/closed the job
      if (signalingChannelRef.current) {
        broadcastOrderStatus(signalingChannelRef.current, {
          sessionId,
          status: "error",
          errorDetail: "The print could not be completed at the counter. Please retry your print.",
        }).catch((err) => console.warn("[App] Error broadcasting error on dismiss:", err));
      }

      try {
        const supabase = getSupabase();
        supabase
          .from("print_orders")
          .update({ status: "failed" })
          .eq("session_id", sessionId)
          .then(() => {});
      } catch {}
    },
    []
  );

  // Loading state
  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div
        className="login-container"
        style={{
          flexDirection: "column",
          gap: 16,
          color: "var(--text-secondary)",
          fontSize: 14,
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            width: 32,
            height: 32,
            border: "2px solid #16181D",
            borderTopColor: "transparent",
            borderRadius: "50%",
            animation: "spin 0.8s linear infinite",
          }}
        />
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)", fontSize: 13, letterSpacing: "0.04em" }}>
          Initializing Terminal...
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Unauthenticated -> Login page
  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <Login />;
  }

  // Authenticated -> Full Shop Admin Shell
  return (
    <div className={`app-shell ${!isSidebarOpen ? "sidebar-collapsed" : ""}`}>
      {isSidebarOpen && (
        <Sidebar
          currentPage={currentPage}
          onSelectPage={handleSelectPage}
          isOnline={isShopOnline}
          onToggleOnline={handleToggleOnline}
          isTogglingOnline={isTogglingOnline}
          onLogoutRequest={handleLogoutRequest}
          onHideSidebar={() => setIsSidebarOpen(false)}
        />
      )}

      <div className="main-workspace">
        <div className="window-drag-strip">
          <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10 }}>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="no-drag sidebar-toggle-btn"
              onClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen((prev) => !prev)}
              title={isSidebarOpen ? "Hide sidebar (Alt+S)" : "Show sidebar (Alt+S)"}
              aria-label={isSidebarOpen ? "Hide sidebar" : "Show sidebar"}
            >
              <IconSidebarToggle size={15} />
              <span className="sidebar-toggle-text">{isSidebarOpen ? "Hide Menu" : "Show Menu"}</span>
            </button>

            <div className="window-drag-title">
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: "#09090b" }}>Printap</span>
              <span style={{ color: "var(--text-faint)" }}>•</span>
              <span style={{ color: "var(--text-secondary)", textTransform: "capitalize" }}>
                {currentPage === "live"
                  ? "Live Monitor"
                  : currentPage === "history"
                  ? "Print History"
                  : currentPage === "printers"
                  ? "Printers Control"
                  : currentPage === "services"
                  ? "Services & Rates"
                  : currentPage === "analytics"
                  ? "Revenue & Analytics"
                  : currentPage === "qr"
                  ? "Counter QR Stand"
                  : "Shop Profile"}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div
            className="no-drag"
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              alignItems: "center",
              gap: 8,
              fontSize: 12,
              fontWeight: 500,
              color:
                isShopOnline && isListening
                  ? "var(--accent-emerald)"
                  : "var(--text-muted)",
            }}
          >
            <span
              className={`beacon-dot ${
                isShopOnline && isListening ? "online" : "offline"
              }`}
              role="status"
              aria-label={
                isShopOnline && isListening
                  ? "Shop Online • P2P Active"
                  : "Shop Offline (Standby)"
              }
              style={{
                background:
                  isShopOnline && isListening
                    ? "var(--accent-emerald)"
                    : "var(--text-muted)",
                boxShadow:
                  isShopOnline && isListening
                    ? "0 0 8px rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.4)"
                    : "none",
              }}
            />
            <span>
              {isShopOnline
                ? isListening
                  ? "Shop Online • P2P Active"
                  : "Connecting P2P..."
                : "Shop Offline (Standby)"}
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Cloud Database Health Badge */}
          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              alignItems: "center",
              gap: 6,
              fontSize: 11,
              fontWeight: 500,
              padding: "3px 8px",
              borderRadius: 6,
              background:
                cloudHealth === "connected"
                  ? "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.08)"
                  : cloudHealth === "checking"
                  ? "rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.08)"
                  : "rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.08)",
              color:
                cloudHealth === "connected"
                  ? "var(--accent-emerald)"
                  : cloudHealth === "checking"
                  ? "#d97706"
                  : "var(--accent-rose)",
            }}
            title={
              cloudHealth === "connected"
                ? "Supabase Cloud: Connected & Syncing"
                : cloudHealth === "checking"
                ? "Verifying Supabase connectivity..."
                : "Supabase Unreachable: Local mutations will sync when restored"
            }
          >
            <span
              style={{
                width: 6,
                height: 6,
                borderRadius: "50%",
                background:
                  cloudHealth === "connected"
                    ? "var(--accent-emerald)"
                    : cloudHealth === "checking"
                    ? "#f59e0b"
                    : "var(--accent-rose)",
              }}
            />
            <span>
              {cloudHealth === "connected"
                ? "Cloud Sync"
                : cloudHealth === "checking"
                ? "Connecting..."
                : "Cloud Disconnected"}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <main style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", overflow: "hidden", position: "relative" }}>
          <div
            style={{
              display: currentPage === "live" ? "flex" : "none",
              flex: 1,
              flexDirection: "column",
              height: "100%",
              width: "100%",
              overflow: "hidden",
            }}
          >
            <LiveDashboard
              currentJob={activeJob}
              recentJobs={recentJobs}
              pendingQueue={pendingQueue}
              todayPrintsCount={todayPrintsCount}
              isListening={isListening}
              connectedPrintersCount={
                printers.filter((p) => p.isPhysical && p.isOnline).length
              }
              onConfirmPayment={handleConfirmPayment}
              onCancelOrder={handleCancelOrder}
              onRetryJob={handleRetryJob}
              onDismissJob={handleDismissJob}
              onNavigateToHistory={() => handleSelectPage("history")}
            />
          </div>

          {currentPage === "history" && (
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100%", width: "100%", overflow: "hidden" }}>
              <PrintHistory recentJobs={recentJobs} />
            </div>
          )}

          {currentPage === "printers" && (
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100%", width: "100%", overflow: "hidden" }}>
              <PrintersControl
                printers={printers}
                isLoadingPrinters={isLoadingPrinters}
                onRescanPrinters={rescanPrinters}
                onAssignmentsSaved={(newAssignments) => {
                  assignmentsRef.current = newAssignments;
                }}
                onRegisterDirtyHandler={(handler) => {
                  dirtyHandlersRef.current["printers"] = handler;
                }}
              />
            </div>
          )}

          {currentPage === "services" && (
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100%", width: "100%", overflow: "hidden" }}>
              <ServicesRates
                onRegisterDirtyHandler={(handler) => {
                  dirtyHandlersRef.current["services"] = handler;
                }}
              />
            </div>
          )}

          {currentPage === "analytics" && (
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100%", width: "100%", overflow: "hidden" }}>
              <Analytics />
            </div>
          )}

          {currentPage === "qr" && (
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100%", width: "100%", overflow: "hidden" }}>
              <CounterQr />
            </div>
          )}

          {currentPage === "profile" && (
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100%", width: "100%", overflow: "hidden" }}>
              <Profile
                onNavigateToQr={() => handleSelectPage("qr")}
                isTerminalOnline={isShopOnline}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </main>
      </div>

      {toastMessage && (
        <div
          style={{
            position: "fixed",
            bottom: 24,
            right: 24,
            background: "#18181b",
            color: "#f4f4f5",
            padding: "10px 16px",
            borderRadius: 10,
            fontSize: 13,
            fontWeight: 500,
            boxShadow: "0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)",
            border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12)",
            zIndex: 9999,
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            gap: 8,
          }}
        >
          <span>⚠️</span>
          <span>{toastMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      <UnsavedChangesModal
        isOpen={Boolean(unsavedModal?.isOpen)}
        pageName={unsavedModal?.pageName || ""}
        isSaving={isSavingUnsaved}
        onCancel={handleCancelNavigation}
        onDiscard={handleDiscardAndContinue}
        onSave={handleSaveAndContinue}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export default function App(): React.ReactElement {
  const [updateState, setUpdateState] = useState<{
    stage: "checking" | "available" | "downloading" | "downloaded" | "error" | "not-available" | null;
    progress?: number;
    error?: string;
    version?: string;
  }>({ stage: "checking" });

  const [startupCheckDone, setStartupCheckDone] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let checkTimeout: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;

    if (window.electronAPI?.onUpdaterEvent) {
      const cleanup = window.electronAPI.onUpdaterEvent((data) => {
        console.log("[App] Updater event:", data.stage, data);
        setUpdateState((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          stage: data.stage as any,
          progress: data.progress,
          error: data.error,
          version: (data.info as any)?.version || prev.version,
        }));

        if (data.stage === "not-available") {
          // Terminal is on the latest release; proceed to load the app
          if (checkTimeout) clearTimeout(checkTimeout);
          setStartupCheckDone(true);
        } else if (data.stage === "error") {
          // Network error or offline during initial check; proceed so offline counter is not blocked
          if (checkTimeout) clearTimeout(checkTimeout);
          console.warn("[App] Updater error on startup check:", data.error);
          setStartupCheckDone(true);
        } else if (data.stage === "available") {
          // Mandatory update available; cancel safety timer and intercept app loading
          if (checkTimeout) clearTimeout(checkTimeout);
          setStartupCheckDone(false);
        }
      });

      // Safety timeout: if after 4.5 seconds no event resolved (e.g. slow network), proceed to app
      checkTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
        setStartupCheckDone((done) => {
          if (!done) {
            console.log("[App] Startup update check timed out; proceeding to app");
            return true;
          }
          return done;
        });
      }, 4500);

      return () => {
        cleanup();
        if (checkTimeout) clearTimeout(checkTimeout);
      };
    } else {
      setStartupCheckDone(true);
    }
  }, []);

  const isMandatoryUpdateActive =
    updateState.stage === "available" ||
    updateState.stage === "downloading" ||
    updateState.stage === "downloaded" ||
    (updateState.stage === "error" && !startupCheckDone);

  // Initial startup screen: checking for updates before loading the counter terminal
  if (!startupCheckDone && !isMandatoryUpdateActive) {
    return (
      <div
        className="login-container"
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          flexDirection: "column",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          height: "100vh",
          width: "100vw",
          backgroundColor: "#F0EBED",
          gap: 16,
          userSelect: "none",
        }}
      >
        <div
          className="login-brand-icon"
          style={{
            width: 52,
            height: 52,
            borderRadius: 14,
            backgroundColor: "#16181D",
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
            boxShadow: "0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.12)",
          }}
        >
          <span style={{ fontSize: 24, color: "#fff", fontWeight: 700 }}>P</span>
        </div>
        <div style={{ textAlign: "center" }}>
          <h1
            style={{
              fontSize: 22,
              fontWeight: 700,
              color: "#16181D",
              fontFamily: "var(--font-sans)",
              letterSpacing: "-0.02em",
              margin: 0,
            }}
          >
            Printap
          </h1>
          <p
            style={{
              fontSize: 13,
              color: "var(--text-secondary)",
              marginTop: 6,
              fontFamily: "var(--font-sans)",
            }}
          >
            Checking for updates...
          </p>
        </div>
        <div style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>
          <div
            style={{
              width: 24,
              height: 24,
              border: "2.5px solid rgba(22, 24, 29, 0.15)",
              borderTopColor: "#16181D",
              borderRadius: "50%",
              animation: "spin 0.75s linear infinite",
            }}
          />
        </div>
        <div
          style={{
            fontSize: 11,
            color: "var(--text-muted)",
            marginTop: 4,
            fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)",
          }}
        >
          Verifying security & release status
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Mandatory update screen: if an update is found on startup, intercept BEFORE loading the app
  if (!startupCheckDone && isMandatoryUpdateActive) {
    return (
      <div
        className="login-container"
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          flexDirection: "column",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          height: "100vh",
          width: "100vw",
          backgroundColor: "#F0EBED",
          userSelect: "none",
        }}
      >
        <UpdateScreen
          stage={updateState.stage as any}
          progress={updateState.progress}
          error={updateState.error}
          version={updateState.version}
          isCompulsory={true}
          onStartDownload={() => window.electronAPI.startDownloadUpdate()}
          onRestart={() => window.electronAPI.restartApp()}
          onDismiss={() => {
            setUpdateState({ stage: null });
            setStartupCheckDone(true);
          }}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <AuthProvider>
        <ShopAdminShell />
      </AuthProvider>
      {isMandatoryUpdateActive && (
        <UpdateScreen
          stage={updateState.stage as any}
          progress={updateState.progress}
          error={updateState.error}
          version={updateState.version}
          isCompulsory={true}
          onStartDownload={() => window.electronAPI.startDownloadUpdate()}
          onRestart={() => window.electronAPI.restartApp()}
          onDismiss={() => {
            setUpdateState({ stage: null });
          }}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [43/62]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/context/AuthContext.tsx
PURPOSE: Authentication Context: Local Session, OTP Reset, Google OAuth
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React, {
  createContext,
  useContext,
  useState,
  useEffect,
  useCallback,
} from "react";
import { getSupabase, sanitizeSubdomain, type ShopInfo } from "@printap/shared";

export type { ShopInfo };

export interface AuthContextType {
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  shopId: string | null;
  shop: ShopInfo | null;
  isLoading: boolean;
  isGoogleSigningIn: boolean;
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
  loginWithGoogle: () => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
  cancelGoogleLogin: () => void;
  reopenGoogleBrowser: () => void;
  logout: () => void;
  refreshShop: () => Promise<void>;
  sendResetOtp: (email: string) => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
  resetPassword: (
    email: string,
    otp: string,
    newPassword: string
  ) => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
}

const STORAGE_KEY = "printap_shop_id";
const SAVED_EMAIL_KEY = "printap_saved_email";
const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || "https://www.mellod.in";

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export function AuthProvider({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}): React.ReactElement {
  const [shopId, setShopId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [shop, setShop] = useState<ShopInfo | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isGoogleSigningIn, setIsGoogleSigningIn] = useState(false);
  const activeGoogleSessionRef = React.useRef<{
    cancel: () => void;
    reopenBrowser: () => void;
  } | null>(null);

  const fetchShopDetails = useCallback(async (idOrSlug: string): Promise<ShopInfo | null> => {
    try {
      const supabase = getSupabase();
      const isUuid =
        /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(
          idOrSlug
        );

      let query = supabase.from("shops").select("*");
      if (isUuid) {
        query = query.eq("id", idOrSlug);
      } else {
        query = query.eq("slug", idOrSlug.toLowerCase());
      }

      const { data, error } = await query.maybeSingle();

      if (error || !data) {
        // Fallback: match by sanitized name if slug is not set
        const { data: allActive } = await supabase
          .from("shops")
          .select("*")
          .eq("status", "active");

        if (allActive && allActive.length > 0) {
          const match = allActive.find(
            (s) => sanitizeSubdomain(s.name) === sanitizeSubdomain(idOrSlug)
          );
          if (match) {
            const resolved = match as ShopInfo;
            if (!resolved.slug) resolved.slug = sanitizeSubdomain(resolved.name);
            return resolved;
          }
        }

        console.warn("[Auth] Failed to load shop:", error?.message);
        return null;
      }

      const resolvedShop = data as ShopInfo;
      // Auto-ensure slug is always populated from shop name
      if (!resolvedShop.slug && resolvedShop.name) {
        resolvedShop.slug = sanitizeSubdomain(resolvedShop.name);
      }

      return resolvedShop;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("[Auth] Error fetching shop details:", err);
      return null;
    }
  }, []);

  // Restore saved shop from localStorage on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const initAuth = async () => {
      const savedId = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (savedId) {
        const shopDetails = await fetchShopDetails(savedId.trim());
        if (shopDetails && shopDetails.status !== "suspended") {
          setShopId(shopDetails.id);
          setShop(shopDetails);
        } else {
          localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        }
      }
      setIsLoading(false);
    };

    initAuth();
  }, [fetchShopDetails]);

  const login = async (
    identifier: string,
    password: string
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
    const cleanIdentifier = identifier.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!cleanIdentifier) {
      return { success: false, error: "Please enter your email or shop subdomain handle." };
    }
    if (!password) {
      return { success: false, error: "Please enter your password." };
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/login`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ identifier: cleanIdentifier, password }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        if (data.unregistered) {
          const onboardUrl = `${API_BASE_URL}/onboard`;
          if (window.electronAPI?.openExternal) {
            window.electronAPI.openExternal(onboardUrl);
          } else {
            window.open(onboardUrl, "_blank");
          }
          return {
            success: false,
            error: "No registered shop found. Redirected to complete onboarding in your browser.",
          };
        }
        return { success: false, error: data.error || "Authentication failed." };
      }

      const shopDetails = data.shop as ShopInfo;
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, shopDetails.id);
      setShopId(shopDetails.id);
      setShop(shopDetails);
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      return { success: false, error: `Connection failed: ${msg}` };
    }
  };

  const sendResetOtp = async (
    email: string
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
    const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!cleanEmail || !cleanEmail.includes("@")) {
      return { success: false, error: "Please enter a valid email address." };
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/send-otp`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email: cleanEmail, purpose: "reset_password" }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        return { success: false, error: data.error || "Failed to send reset code." };
      }
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      return { success: false, error: `Network error: ${msg}` };
    }
  };

  const resetPassword = async (
    email: string,
    otp: string,
    newPassword: string
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/reset-password`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
          otp: otp.trim(),
          newPassword,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        return { success: false, error: data.error || "Password reset failed." };
      }
      return { success: true };
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      return { success: false, error: `Network error: ${msg}` };
    }
  };

  const loginWithGoogle = async (): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
    const sessionId = crypto.randomUUID();
    setIsGoogleSigningIn(true);

    const authUrl = `${API_BASE_URL}/auth/desktop-login?session=${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}`;

    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      let settled = false;

      const cleanup = () => {
        if (channel) {
          try {
            channel.unsubscribe();
          } catch {}
        }
        if (pollTimer) clearInterval(pollTimer);
        if (deepLinkCleanup) deepLinkCleanup();
        setIsGoogleSigningIn(false);
        activeGoogleSessionRef.current = null;
      };

      const onSuccess = async (shopData: ShopInfo) => {
        if (settled) return;
        settled = true;
        cleanup();
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, shopData.id);
        setShopId(shopData.id);
        setShop(shopData);
        resolve({ success: true });
      };

      const onError = (error: string) => {
        if (settled) return;
        settled = true;
        cleanup();
        resolve({ success: false, error });
      };

      // 1. Listen via Supabase Realtime Broadcast Channel
      const supabase = getSupabase();
      const channel = supabase.channel(`desktop-auth:${sessionId}`, {
        config: { broadcast: { self: false } },
      });

      channel.on("broadcast", { event: "login-success" }, async ({ payload }) => {
        console.log("[Auth] Google login broadcast success:", payload);
        const p = payload as { shopId?: string; shop?: ShopInfo };
        if (p.shop) {
          onSuccess(p.shop);
        } else if (p.shopId) {
          const s = await fetchShopDetails(p.shopId);
          if (s) onSuccess(s);
          else onError("Failed to load shop details after login.");
        }
      });

      channel.on("broadcast", { event: "login-error" }, ({ payload }) => {
        const p = payload as { error?: string };
        onError(p.error || "Google authentication failed.");
      });

      channel.subscribe();

      // 2. Secondary Fallback: Poll desktop-session API every 1.5s
      const pollTimer = setInterval(async () => {
        if (settled) return;
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            `${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/desktop-session?session=${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}`
          );
          if (res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.status === "authenticated" && data.shop) {
              onSuccess(data.shop as ShopInfo);
            } else if (data.status === "unregistered") {
              onError(
                data.error ||
                  "No registered shop found. Redirected to complete onboarding in your browser."
              );
            }
          }
        } catch {}
      }, 1500);

      // 3. Tertiary: Deep-link listener from Electron main process
      let deepLinkCleanup: (() => void) | null = null;
      if (window.electronAPI?.onDeepLinkAuth) {
        deepLinkCleanup = window.electronAPI.onDeepLinkAuth(
          async ({ sessionId: linkSession, shopId: linkShopId }) => {
            if (linkSession === sessionId && linkShopId) {
              const s = await fetchShopDetails(linkShopId);
              if (s) onSuccess(s);
            }
          }
        );
      }

      // Open external system browser
      if (window.electronAPI?.openExternal) {
        window.electronAPI.openExternal(authUrl);
      } else {
        window.open(authUrl, "_blank");
      }

      // Store controller for cancellation or re-opening
      activeGoogleSessionRef.current = {
        cancel: () => {
          if (!settled) {
            settled = true;
            cleanup();
            resolve({ success: false, error: "Login cancelled by user." });
          }
        },
        reopenBrowser: () => {
          if (window.electronAPI?.openExternal) {
            window.electronAPI.openExternal(authUrl);
          } else {
            window.open(authUrl, "_blank");
          }
        },
      };
    });
  };

  const cancelGoogleLogin = () => {
    activeGoogleSessionRef.current?.cancel();
  };

  const reopenGoogleBrowser = () => {
    activeGoogleSessionRef.current?.reopenBrowser();
  };

  const logout = () => {
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    setShopId(null);
    setShop(null);
  };

  // Cancel active Google OAuth polling session on unmount
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      activeGoogleSessionRef.current?.cancel();
    };
  }, []);

  const refreshShop = async () => {
    if (!shopId) return;
    const updated = await fetchShopDetails(shopId);
    if (updated) setShop(updated);
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        isAuthenticated: !!shopId && !!shop,
        shopId,
        shop,
        isLoading,
        isGoogleSigningIn,
        login,
        loginWithGoogle,
        cancelGoogleLogin,
        reopenGoogleBrowser,
        logout,
        refreshShop,
        sendResetOtp,
        resetPassword,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth(): AuthContextType {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider");
  }
  return context;
}


───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [44/62]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/lib/shop-signaling.ts
PURPOSE: WebRTC Receiver & Signaling Channel: SDP Handshake, Chunk Assembler
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import {
  joinSignalingChannel,
  leaveSignalingChannel,
  broadcast,
  DEFAULT_ICE_SERVERS,
  waitForIceGathering,
  type SignalPayload,
  type PrintSettings,
  type TransferManifestOrder,
  type TransferFileHeaderMessage,
} from "@printap/shared";
import type { RealtimeChannel } from "@supabase/supabase-js";

export interface ShopSession {
  sessionId: string;
  pc: RTCPeerConnection;
  channel: RealtimeChannel;
  dataChannel: RTCDataChannel | null;
}

const MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES = 100 * 1024 * 1024; // 100MB hard cap per file
const MAX_ORDER_TOTAL_BYTES = 250 * 1024 * 1024; // 250MB hard cap per order
const PROGRESS_THROTTLE_MS = 100; // Max 10 UI updates per second

const sessions = new Map<string, ShopSession>();
const earlyCandidates = new Map<string, RTCIceCandidateInit[]>();
const earlyCandidateTimers = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();
const sessionInactivityTimers = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();

const offerTimestamps = new Map<string, number[]>();
const OFFER_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS = 10_000;
const MAX_OFFERS_PER_WINDOW = 3;
let shopReconnectTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
let shopReconnectAttempts = 0;

let activeSignalingShopId: string | null = null;

function resetSessionTimeout(sessionId: string, timeoutMs = 90_000): void {
  const existing = sessionInactivityTimers.get(sessionId);
  if (existing) clearTimeout(existing);
  sessionInactivityTimers.set(
    sessionId,
    setTimeout(() => {
      console.log(`[shop] Session ${sessionId} timed out due to inactivity`);
      cleanup(sessionId);
    }, timeoutMs)
  );
}

async function computeSha256Hex(buffer: ArrayBuffer): Promise<string> {
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", buffer);
  const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
  return hashArray.map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

export interface ReceivedOrderFile {
  fileId: string;
  fileName: string;
  fileSize: number;
  pageCount: number;
  settings: PrintSettings;
  buffer: ArrayBuffer;
}

export interface ReceivedOrder {
  sessionId: string;
  order: TransferManifestOrder;
  files: ReceivedOrderFile[];
}

export type FileReceivedCallback = (
  sessionId: string,
  fileBuffer: ArrayBuffer,
  fileName?: string
) => void;

export type OrderReceivedCallback = (order: ReceivedOrder) => void;

export interface SignalingCallbacks {
  onSessionStart?: (sessionId: string) => void;
  onRequestToken?: (
    sessionId: string,
    hasColor: boolean,
    totalAmount?: number,
    fileCount?: number
  ) => void;
  onFileProgress?: (
    sessionId: string,
    fileName: string,
    pct: number,
    received: number,
    total: number,
    fileIndex?: number,
    totalFiles?: number
  ) => void;
  onFileReceived: FileReceivedCallback;
  onManifestReceived?: (sessionId: string, order: TransferManifestOrder) => void;
  onOrderReceived?: OrderReceivedCallback;
  onSessionError?: (sessionId: string, error: string) => void;
}

export function startShopSignaling(
  shopId: string,
  callbacksOrHandler: FileReceivedCallback | SignalingCallbacks
): RealtimeChannel {
  const callbacks: SignalingCallbacks =
    typeof callbacksOrHandler === "function"
      ? { onFileReceived: callbacksOrHandler }
      : callbacksOrHandler;

  activeSignalingShopId = shopId;
  shopReconnectAttempts = 0;
  if (shopReconnectTimer) {
    clearTimeout(shopReconnectTimer);
    shopReconnectTimer = null;
  }

  function setupChannel(): RealtimeChannel {
    const channel = joinSignalingChannel(shopId, true);

    channel.on(
      "broadcast",
      { event: "webrtc-offer" },
      async ({ payload }: { payload: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
        const { sessionId, data } = payload as unknown as SignalPayload;
        if (!sessionId || !data) return;

        // Rate limit incoming offers per sessionId (prevent DoS)
        const now = Date.now();
        const recentOffers = (offerTimestamps.get(sessionId) || []).filter(
          (t) => now - t < OFFER_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS
        );
        if (recentOffers.length >= MAX_OFFERS_PER_WINDOW) {
          console.warn(
            `[shop] Rate-limited offer for session ${sessionId} (${recentOffers.length} offers in ${OFFER_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS}ms)`
          );
          return;
        }
        recentOffers.push(now);
        offerTimestamps.set(sessionId, recentOffers);

        console.log(`[shop] Received offer for session ${sessionId}`);
        callbacks.onSessionStart?.(sessionId);
        await handleOffer(
          channel,
          sessionId,
          data as RTCSessionDescriptionInit,
          callbacks
        );
      }
    );

    channel.on(
      "broadcast",
      { event: "webrtc-ice" },
      async ({ payload }: { payload: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
        const { sessionId, data } = payload as unknown as SignalPayload;
        const candidateInit = data as RTCIceCandidateInit;
        if (!sessionId || !candidateInit || !candidateInit.candidate) return;
        const session = sessions.get(sessionId);

        if (!session || !session.pc.remoteDescription) {
          if (!earlyCandidates.has(sessionId)) {
            earlyCandidates.set(sessionId, []);
          }
          const queue = earlyCandidates.get(sessionId)!;
          if (queue.length < 50) {
            queue.push(candidateInit);
          }
          if (!earlyCandidateTimers.has(sessionId)) {
            earlyCandidateTimers.set(
              sessionId,
              setTimeout(() => {
                earlyCandidates.delete(sessionId);
                earlyCandidateTimers.delete(sessionId);
              }, 60_000)
            );
          }
          return;
        }

        try {
          await session.pc.addIceCandidate(new RTCIceCandidate(candidateInit));
        } catch (err) {
          console.error(`[shop] Failed to add ICE candidate:`, err);
        }
      }
    );

    channel.on(
      "broadcast",
      { event: "request-token" },
      ({ payload }: { payload: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
        const { sessionId, hasColor, totalAmount, fileCount } =
          payload as unknown as SignalPayload;
        if (!sessionId) return;
        callbacks.onRequestToken?.(
          sessionId,
          Boolean(hasColor),
          totalAmount,
          fileCount
        );
      }
    );

    channel.on(
      "broadcast",
      { event: "order-status" },
      ({ payload }: { payload: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
        const { sessionId, status, errorDetail } =
          payload as unknown as SignalPayload;
        if (!sessionId) return;
        if (status === "cancelled" || status === "error") {
          callbacks.onSessionError?.(
            sessionId,
            errorDetail || `Customer order was ${status}`
          );
          cleanup(sessionId);
        }
      }
    );

    // Channel lifecycle reconnection handling
    channel.subscribe((status) => {
      console.log(`[shop] Signaling subscription status: ${status}`);
      if (status === "SUBSCRIBED") {
        shopReconnectAttempts = 0;
      } else if (
        status === "CHANNEL_ERROR" ||
        status === "TIMED_OUT" ||
        status === "CLOSED"
      ) {
        if (activeSignalingShopId === shopId) {
          const backoffMs = Math.min(
            1000 * Math.pow(1.5, shopReconnectAttempts),
            10_000
          );
          shopReconnectAttempts++;
          console.log(
            `[shop] Channel dropped (${status}). Reconnecting in ${Math.round(
              backoffMs
            )}ms (attempt ${shopReconnectAttempts})...`
          );
          if (shopReconnectTimer) clearTimeout(shopReconnectTimer);
          shopReconnectTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            if (activeSignalingShopId === shopId) {
              setupChannel();
            }
          }, backoffMs);
        }
      }
    });

    return channel;
  }

  const initialChannel = setupChannel();
  console.log(`[shop] Signaling started for shop ${shopId}`);
  return initialChannel;
}

export function stopShopSignaling(channel: RealtimeChannel | null): void {
  if (shopReconnectTimer) {
    clearTimeout(shopReconnectTimer);
    shopReconnectTimer = null;
  }
  shopReconnectAttempts = 0;
  offerTimestamps.clear();

  if (activeSignalingShopId) {
    leaveSignalingChannel(activeSignalingShopId).catch(() => {});
    activeSignalingShopId = null;
  } else if (channel) {
    try {
      channel.unsubscribe();
    } catch {}
  }

  for (const timer of earlyCandidateTimers.values()) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
  earlyCandidateTimers.clear();
  earlyCandidates.clear();

  for (const timer of sessionInactivityTimers.values()) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
  sessionInactivityTimers.clear();

  for (const sessionId of Array.from(sessions.keys())) {
    cleanup(sessionId);
  }
}

async function handleOffer(
  channel: RealtimeChannel,
  sessionId: string,
  offer: RTCSessionDescriptionInit,
  callbacks: SignalingCallbacks
): Promise<void> {
  const existing = sessions.get(sessionId);
  if (existing) {
    if (
      existing.pc.connectionState === "connected" ||
      existing.pc.connectionState === "connecting" ||
      existing.pc.signalingState === "stable"
    ) {
      if (existing.pc.localDescription) {
        const localDesc = existing.pc.localDescription;
        await broadcast(channel, "webrtc-answer", {
          sessionId,
          data: { type: localDesc.type, sdp: localDesc.sdp },
        });
      }
      return;
    } else {
      cleanup(sessionId);
    }
  }

  resetSessionTimeout(sessionId);

  const pc = new RTCPeerConnection({
    iceServers: DEFAULT_ICE_SERVERS,
    iceCandidatePoolSize: 10,
  });

  const session: ShopSession = {
    sessionId,
    pc,
    channel,
    dataChannel: null,
  };
  sessions.set(sessionId, session);

  pc.onicecandidate = (event) => {
    if (event.candidate && event.candidate.candidate) {
      broadcast(channel, "webrtc-ice", {
        sessionId,
        data: event.candidate.toJSON(),
      });
    }
  };

  pc.onconnectionstatechange = () => {
    if (pc.connectionState === "failed" || pc.connectionState === "closed") {
      callbacks.onSessionError?.(
        sessionId,
        "WebRTC peer connection closed or failed"
      );
      cleanup(sessionId);
    }
  };

  pc.ondatachannel = (event) => {
    session.dataChannel = event.channel;
    setupDataChannelReceiver(session, callbacks);
  };

  try {
    await pc.setRemoteDescription(new RTCSessionDescription(offer));

    const buffered = earlyCandidates.get(sessionId);
    if (buffered && buffered.length > 0) {
      for (const cand of buffered) {
        try {
          if (cand && cand.candidate) {
            await pc.addIceCandidate(new RTCIceCandidate(cand));
          }
        } catch {}
      }
      earlyCandidates.delete(sessionId);
    }

    const answer = await pc.createAnswer();
    await pc.setLocalDescription(answer);
    await waitForIceGathering(pc, 800);

    const localAnswer = pc.localDescription || answer;
    await broadcast(channel, "webrtc-answer", {
      sessionId,
      data: { type: localAnswer.type, sdp: localAnswer.sdp },
    });
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
    callbacks.onSessionError?.(sessionId, msg);
    cleanup(sessionId);
  }
}

function setupDataChannelReceiver(
  session: ShopSession,
  callbacks: SignalingCallbacks
): void {
  const dc = session.dataChannel!;
  dc.binaryType = "arraybuffer";

  let manifest: TransferManifestOrder | null = null;
  const receivedFiles: ReceivedOrderFile[] = [];
  let orderTotalBytesReceived = 0;

  let currentFileHeader: TransferFileHeaderMessage | null = null;
  let legacyMetadata: { fileName: string; fileSize: number; totalChunks: number } | null = null;

  let assemblyBuffer: Uint8Array | null = null;
  let expectedSize = 0;
  let receivedSize = 0;
  let lastProgressEmitTime = 0;

  const finalizeCurrentFile = (): ArrayBuffer | null => {
    if (!assemblyBuffer || receivedSize === 0) return null;
    const rawBuffer = assemblyBuffer.buffer as ArrayBuffer;
    const finalBuffer =
      receivedSize === assemblyBuffer.byteLength
        ? rawBuffer
        : rawBuffer.slice(0, receivedSize);
    assemblyBuffer = null;
    return finalBuffer;
  };

  dc.onmessage = async (event) => {
    resetSessionTimeout(session.sessionId);

    if (typeof event.data === "string") {
      if (event.data === "EOF") {
        const finalBuf = finalizeCurrentFile();
        if (finalBuf) {
          if (currentFileHeader && (currentFileHeader as any).sha256) {
            const actualHash = await computeSha256Hex(finalBuf);
            if (actualHash !== (currentFileHeader as any).sha256) {
              callbacks.onSessionError?.(
                session.sessionId,
                `Integrity check failed for ${currentFileHeader.fileName}`
              );
              cleanup(session.sessionId);
              return;
            }
          }

          const fileName =
            currentFileHeader?.fileName ||
            legacyMetadata?.fileName ||
            `file_${receivedFiles.length + 1}`;

          if (currentFileHeader) {
            const alreadyPushed = receivedFiles.some(
              (f) => f.fileId === currentFileHeader!.fileId
            );
            if (!alreadyPushed) {
              receivedFiles.push({
                fileId: currentFileHeader.fileId,
                fileName: currentFileHeader.fileName,
                fileSize: finalBuf.byteLength,
                pageCount: currentFileHeader.pageCount,
                settings: currentFileHeader.settings,
                buffer: finalBuf,
              });
            }
          }

          if (!manifest) {
            callbacks.onFileReceived(session.sessionId, finalBuf, fileName);
          }

          receivedSize = 0;
          expectedSize = 0;
          legacyMetadata = null;
        }
        return;
      }

      try {
        const msg = JSON.parse(event.data);

        if (msg.type === "order-manifest") {
          manifest = msg.order;
          callbacks.onManifestReceived?.(session.sessionId, msg.order);
          return;
        }

        if (msg.type === "file-header") {
          if (
            typeof msg.fileSize !== "number" ||
            msg.fileSize <= 0 ||
            msg.fileSize > MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES
          ) {
            callbacks.onSessionError?.(
