───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [55/62]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/components/Login.tsx
PURPOSE: Shopkeeper Counter Terminal Authentication & Password Reset
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { IconShield, IconCheck } from "./Icons";
import { validateEmail } from "@printap/shared";

const SAVED_EMAIL_KEY = "printap_saved_email";

export const Login: React.FC = () => {
  const {
    login,
    sendResetOtp,
    resetPassword,
  } = useAuth();

  // Login form state
  const [emailInput, setEmailInput] = useState("");
  const [passwordInput, setPasswordInput] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [rememberEmail, setRememberEmail] = useState(true);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Forgot Password modal state
  const [showForgotModal, setShowForgotModal] = useState(false);
  const [forgotStep, setForgotStep] = useState<1 | 2>(1);
  const [forgotEmail, setForgotEmail] = useState("");
  const [forgotOtp, setForgotOtp] = useState("");
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmNewPassword, setConfirmNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [forgotLoading, setForgotLoading] = useState(false);
  const [forgotError, setForgotError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [forgotSuccess, setForgotSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [resendCooldown, setResendCooldown] = useState(0);

  // Load saved email on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(SAVED_EMAIL_KEY);
    if (saved) {
      setEmailInput(saved);
      setForgotEmail(saved);
    }
  }, []);

  // Cooldown countdown timer
  useEffect(() => {
    if (resendCooldown <= 0) return;
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setResendCooldown((prev) => (prev > 0 ? prev - 1 : 0));
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [resendCooldown]);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const cleanId = emailInput.trim();
    if (!cleanId) {
      setErrorMessage("Please enter your email address or shop handle.");
      return;
    }
    if (!passwordInput) {
      setErrorMessage("Please enter your password.");
      return;
    }

    setIsSubmitting(true);
    setErrorMessage(null);

    const result = await login(cleanId, passwordInput);
    if (!result.success) {
      setErrorMessage(result.error || "Terminal authentication failed.");
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    } else {
      if (rememberEmail) {
        localStorage.setItem(SAVED_EMAIL_KEY, cleanId);
      } else {
        localStorage.removeItem(SAVED_EMAIL_KEY);
      }
    }
  };


  // Step 1: Send OTP for password reset
  const handleSendResetCode = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const emailCheck = validateEmail(forgotEmail);
    if (!emailCheck.valid) {
      setForgotError(emailCheck.reason || "Please enter a valid registered email address.");
      return;
    }

    setForgotLoading(true);
    setForgotError(null);

    const res = await sendResetOtp(emailCheck.normalizedEmail);
    if (res.success) {
      setForgotStep(2);
      setResendCooldown(60);
    } else {
      setForgotError(res.error || "Failed to send reset code.");
    }
    setForgotLoading(false);
  };

  // Step 2: Confirm OTP & set new password
  const handleResetPassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (forgotOtp.trim().length !== 6) {
      setForgotError("Please enter the 6-digit verification code.");
      return;
    }
    if (newPassword.length < 8) {
      setForgotError("Password must be at least 8 characters long.");
      return;
    }
    if (newPassword !== confirmNewPassword) {
      setForgotError("Passwords do not match.");
      return;
    }

    setForgotLoading(true);
    setForgotError(null);

    const res = await resetPassword(forgotEmail.trim(), forgotOtp.trim(), newPassword);
    if (res.success) {
      setForgotSuccess("Password reset successfully! You can now log in.");
      setEmailInput(forgotEmail.trim());
      setPasswordInput(newPassword);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setShowForgotModal(false);
        setForgotStep(1);
        setForgotOtp("");
        setNewPassword("");
        setConfirmNewPassword("");
        setForgotSuccess(null);
      }, 1500);
    } else {
      setForgotError(res.error || "Password reset failed.");
    }
    setForgotLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="login-container">
      <div className="login-card fade-in">
        <h1 className="login-title">Counter Terminal</h1>
        <p className="login-subtitle">
          Sign in with your registered shop account to connect your counter terminal.
        </p>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{ marginTop: 24 }}>
          {/* Email Input */}
          <div style={{ textAlign: "left", marginBottom: 6 }}>
            <label
              style={{
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: "var(--text-secondary)",
                display: "block",
              }}
            >
              Shop Email or Subdomain Handle
            </label>
          </div>
          <input
            type="text"
            className="login-input"
            placeholder="e.g. rahul@gmail.com or apex-prints"
            value={emailInput}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setEmailInput(e.target.value);
              if (errorMessage) setErrorMessage(null);
            }}
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            autoFocus
            required
          />

          {/* Password Input */}
          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              justifyContent: "space-between",
              alignItems: "center",
              marginTop: 14,
              marginBottom: 6,
            }}
          >
            <label
              style={{
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: "var(--text-secondary)",
              }}
            >
              Terminal Password
            </label>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
              style={{
                background: "none",
                border: "none",
                fontSize: 11,
                color: "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)",
                cursor: "pointer",
                padding: 0,
              }}
            >
              {showPassword ? "Hide" : "Show"}
            </button>
          </div>
          <input
            type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
            className="login-input"
            placeholder="Enter your password"
            value={passwordInput}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setPasswordInput(e.target.value);
              if (errorMessage) setErrorMessage(null);
            }}
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            required
          />

          {/* Remember Email & Forgot Password */}
          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              justifyContent: "space-between",
              alignItems: "center",
              marginTop: 12,
              fontSize: 12,
            }}
          >
            <label
              style={{
                display: "flex",
                alignItems: "center",
                gap: 6,
                cursor: "pointer",
                color: "var(--text-muted)",
              }}
            >
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={rememberEmail}
                onChange={(e) => setRememberEmail(e.target.checked)}
                style={{ cursor: "pointer", accentColor: "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)" }}
              />
              Remember Email
            </label>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setShowForgotModal(true);
                setForgotEmail(emailInput || "");
                setForgotError(null);
              }}
              style={{
                background: "none",
                border: "none",
                color: "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)",
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: 600,
                cursor: "pointer",
                padding: 0,
              }}
            >
              Forgot Password?
            </button>
          </div>

          {errorMessage && (
            <div className="login-error" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
              <div>{errorMessage}</div>
              {errorMessage.toLowerCase().includes("onboard") && (
                <div style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      const onboardUrl = "https://www.mellod.in/onboard";
                      if (window.electronAPI?.openExternal) {
                        window.electronAPI.openExternal(onboardUrl);
                      } else {
                        window.open(onboardUrl, "_blank");
                      }
                    }}
                    style={{
                      background: "none",
                      border: "none",
                      color: "#fff",
                      textDecoration: "underline",
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: 700,
                      cursor: "pointer",
                      padding: 0,
                    }}
                  >
                    Open Onboarding in Browser →
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Connect Button */}
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="login-btn"
            style={{ marginTop: 20 }}
            disabled={isSubmitting || !emailInput.trim() || !passwordInput}
          >
            {isSubmitting ? "Connecting Terminal..." : "Connect Counter Terminal"}
          </button>
        </form>

        {/* Onboarding Link */}
        <div style={{ marginTop: 16, fontSize: 12, color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>
          Need to onboard a new shop?{" "}
          <a
            href="https://www.mellod.in/onboard"
            target="_blank"
            rel="noreferrer"
            style={{
              color: "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)",
              fontWeight: 600,
              textDecoration: "none",
            }}
          >
            Register on Web →
          </a>
        </div>

        {/* Trust Badges */}
        <div
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
            gap: 8,
            marginTop: 24,
            fontSize: 12,
            color: "var(--text-muted)",
          }}
        >
          <IconShield size={14} color="var(--accent-emerald)" />
          <span>Zero Cloud Storage • Direct Peer-to-Peer</span>
        </div>

        {/* Legal links */}
        <div
          style={{
            marginTop: 16,
            fontSize: 11,
            color: "var(--text-muted)",
            display: "flex",
            justifyContent: "center",
            gap: 12,
          }}
        >
          <a
            href="https://mellod.in/terms"
            target="_blank"
            rel="noreferrer"
            style={{ color: "var(--text-muted)", textDecoration: "underline" }}
          >
            Terms
          </a>
          <span>•</span>
          <a
            href="https://mellod.in/privacy"
            target="_blank"
            rel="noreferrer"
            style={{ color: "var(--text-muted)", textDecoration: "underline" }}
          >
            Privacy Policy
          </a>
          <span>•</span>
          <a
            href="https://mellod.in/refund-policy"
            target="_blank"
            rel="noreferrer"
            style={{ color: "var(--text-muted)", textDecoration: "underline" }}
          >
            Refunds
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* MODAL: FORGOT & RESET PASSWORD */}
      {showForgotModal && (
        <div
          style={{
            position: "fixed",
            inset: 0,
            background: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.75)",
            backdropFilter: "blur(4px)",
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
            zIndex: 1000,
            padding: 16,
          }}
        >
          <div
            className="login-card fade-in"
            style={{
              maxWidth: 400,
              width: "100%",
              margin: 0,
              background: "var(--surface-primary, #1e1e24)",
              border: "1px solid var(--border-subtle, #333)",
            }}
          >
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700, margin: "0 0 6px 0", color: "#fff" }}>
              Reset Terminal Password
            </h2>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "var(--text-muted)", margin: "0 0 16px 0" }}>
              {forgotStep === 1
                ? "Enter your registered shop owner email to receive a 6-digit reset code."
                : `Enter the 6-digit code sent to ${forgotEmail} and choose a new password.`}
            </p>

            {forgotError && (
              <div className="login-error" style={{ marginBottom: 14 }}>
                {forgotError}
              </div>
            )}
            {forgotSuccess && (
              <div
                style={{
                  background: "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.15)",
                  border: "1px solid #10b981",
                  color: "#34d399",
                  padding: 10,
                  borderRadius: 8,
                  fontSize: 12,
                  marginBottom: 14,
                  display: "flex",
                  alignItems: "center",
                  gap: 6,
                }}
              >
                <IconCheck size={14} />
                <span>{forgotSuccess}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {forgotStep === 1 ? (
              <form onSubmit={handleSendResetCode}>
                <input
                  type="email"
                  className="login-input"
                  placeholder="Enter registered email"
                  value={forgotEmail}
                  onChange={(e) => setForgotEmail(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={forgotLoading}
                  autoFocus
                  required
                />
                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginTop: 16 }}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="btn-ghost"
                    style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}
                    onClick={() => setShowForgotModal(false)}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                  >
                    Cancel
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    className="login-btn"
                    style={{ flex: 1, marginTop: 0 }}
                    disabled={forgotLoading || !forgotEmail.trim()}
                  >
                    {forgotLoading ? "Sending Code..." : "Send Reset Code"}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleResetPassword}>
                <div style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}>
                  <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-secondary)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>
                    6-Digit Verification Code
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    maxLength={6}
                    className="login-input"
                    placeholder="e.g. 123456"
                    value={forgotOtp}
                    onChange={(e) => setForgotOtp(e.target.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""))}
                    style={{ letterSpacing: 4, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 18 }}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                    autoFocus
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}>
                  <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-secondary)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>
                    New Password (min. 8 characters)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    className="login-input"
                    placeholder="New password"
                    value={newPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div style={{ marginBottom: 14 }}>
                  <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-secondary)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>
                    Confirm New Password
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    className="login-input"
                    placeholder="Confirm new password"
                    value={confirmNewPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setConfirmNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 14, fontSize: 11 }}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setForgotStep(1)}
                    style={{ background: "none", border: "none", color: "var(--text-muted)", cursor: "pointer", padding: 0 }}
                  >
                    ← Change Email
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={async () => {
                      if (resendCooldown > 0) return;
                      await sendResetOtp(forgotEmail.trim());
                      setResendCooldown(60);
                    }}
                    disabled={resendCooldown > 0 || forgotLoading}
                    style={{
                      background: "none",
                      border: "none",
                      color: resendCooldown > 0 ? "var(--text-muted)" : "var(--accent-primary, #6366f1)",
                      cursor: resendCooldown > 0 ? "default" : "pointer",
                      padding: 0,
                      fontWeight: 600,
                    }}
                  >
                    {resendCooldown > 0 ? `Resend code in ${resendCooldown}s` : "Resend Code"}
                  </button>
                </div>

                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="btn-ghost"
                    style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" }}
                    onClick={() => setShowForgotModal(false)}
                    disabled={forgotLoading}
                  >
                    Cancel
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    className="login-btn"
                    style={{ flex: 1, marginTop: 0 }}
                    disabled={forgotLoading || forgotOtp.length !== 6 || newPassword.length < 8}
                  >
                    {forgotLoading ? "Resetting..." : "Set New Password"}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

PURPOSE: Software Auto-Updater Modal (Download Progress & Restart)

import React from "react";

export type UpdateStage = "checking" | "available" | "downloading" | "downloaded" | "error" | "not-available" | null;

interface UpdateScreenProps {
  stage: UpdateStage;
  progress?: number;
  error?: string;
  version?: string;
  onStartDownload: () => void;
  onRestart: () => void;
  onDismiss?: () => void;
  isCompulsory?: boolean;
}

export const UpdateScreen: React.FC<UpdateScreenProps> = ({
  stage,
  progress = 0,
  error,
  version,
  onStartDownload,
  onRestart,
  onDismiss,
  isCompulsory = true,
}) => {
  if (!stage || stage === "not-available" || stage === "checking") return null;

  return (
    <div className="modal-overlay" style={{ zIndex: 10000 }}>
      <div
        className="confirm-dialog-card"
        style={{
          maxWidth: stage === "error" ? 640 : 440,
          width: stage === "error" ? "min(640px, 92vw)" : "min(440px, 92vw)",
          maxHeight: "85vh",
          display: "flex",
          flexDirection: "column",
          boxSizing: "border-box",
          borderRadius: "var(--radius-lg, 12px)",
          boxShadow: "0 24px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25)",
        }}
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* State 1: Update Available (Compulsory) */}
        {stage === "available" && (
          <>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12, marginBottom: 14 }}>
              <div
                style={{
                  width: 40,
                  height: 40,
                  borderRadius: "50%",
                  background: "#FEF3C7",
                  border: "1px solid #FDE68A",
                  display: "flex",
                  alignItems: "center",
                  justifyContent: "center",
                  color: "#D97706",
                  fontSize: 20,
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  flexShrink: 0,
                }}
              >
                !
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="confirm-dialog-title" style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 16 }}>
                  Mandatory Update Required
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-muted)", fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)", marginTop: 2 }}>
                  {version ? `New Version: v${version}` : "New release available"}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="confirm-dialog-body" style={{ marginBottom: 22, fontSize: 13, lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
              A required update is available for the Printap Terminal. To maintain secure counter printing, database synchronization, and encryption standards, this update must be installed to continue.
            </div>

            <div className="confirm-dialog-actions" style={{ flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn-dialog-confirm"
                style={{
                  width: "100%",
                  padding: "12px 18px",
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm, 8px)",
                  display: "flex",
                  alignItems: "center",
                  justifyContent: "center",
                  gap: 8,
                }}
                onClick={onStartDownload}
              >
                <span>Download & Install Update</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* State 2: Downloading */}
        {stage === "downloading" && (
          <>
            <div className="confirm-dialog-title" style={{ fontSize: 16, marginBottom: 8 }}>
              Downloading Update Package...
            </div>
            <div className="confirm-dialog-body" style={{ marginBottom: 20, textAlign: "center" }}>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "var(--text-secondary)", marginBottom: 16 }}>
                Please keep the application open. Downloading verified files from the release server.
              </div>
              
              <div
                style={{
                  width: "100%",
                  height: 8,
                  background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.06)",
                  borderRadius: 9999,
                  overflow: "hidden",
                  marginBottom: 10,
                }}
              >
                <div 
                  style={{ 
                    height: "100%", 
                    width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, progress))}%`, 
                    background: "var(--accent-emerald, #10b981)",
                    borderRadius: 9999,
                    transition: "width 0.2s ease-out",
                  }} 
                />
              </div>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700, fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)", color: "var(--text-primary)" }}>
                {Math.round(progress)}%
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* State 3: Downloaded & Ready to Restart (Compulsory) */}
        {stage === "downloaded" && (
          <>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12, marginBottom: 14 }}>
              <div
                style={{
                  width: 40,
                  height: 40,
                  borderRadius: "50%",
                  background: "#D1FAE5",
                  border: "1px solid #A7F3D0",
                  display: "flex",
                  alignItems: "center",
                  justifyContent: "center",
                  color: "#059669",
                  fontSize: 20,
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  flexShrink: 0,
                }}
              >
                ✓
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="confirm-dialog-title" style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 16, color: "var(--accent-emerald, #10b981)" }}>
                  Update Ready to Apply
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--text-muted)", fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)", marginTop: 2 }}>
                  Package verified
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="confirm-dialog-body" style={{ marginBottom: 22, fontSize: 13, lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
              The update has been downloaded. The terminal application will restart now to apply the updates and complete installation.
            </div>

            <div className="confirm-dialog-actions" style={{ flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn-dialog-confirm"
                style={{
                  width: "100%",
                  padding: "12px 18px",
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  background: "var(--accent-emerald, #10b981)",
                  borderColor: "var(--accent-emerald, #10b981)",
                  borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm, 8px)",
                  display: "flex",
                  alignItems: "center",
                  justifyContent: "center",
                  gap: 8,
                }}
                onClick={onRestart}
              >
                <span>Restart & Apply Update Now</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* State 4: Error */}
        {stage === "error" && (
          <>
            <div className="confirm-dialog-title" style={{ color: "var(--accent-rose)", flexShrink: 0, fontSize: 16 }}>
              Update Failed
            </div>
            <div
              className="confirm-dialog-body"
              style={{
                marginBottom: 18,
                flex: "1 1 auto",
                minHeight: 0,
                display: "flex",
                flexDirection: "column",
              }}
            >
              <div style={{ marginBottom: 8, flexShrink: 0, fontSize: 13, color: "var(--text-secondary)" }}>
                An error occurred while downloading the update:
              </div>
              <div
                style={{
                  padding: "10px 12px",
                  background: "rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.08)",
                  border: "1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.2)",
                  borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm, 8px)",
                  fontSize: 11,
                  lineHeight: 1.5,
                  color: "var(--accent-rose)",
                  fontFamily: "ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, monospace",
                  wordBreak: "break-all",
                  whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
                  overflowY: "auto",
                  maxHeight: "180px",
                }}
              >
                {error || "Unknown network error"}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="confirm-dialog-actions" style={{ flexShrink: 0, marginTop: "auto", display: "flex", gap: 10 }}>
              {!isCompulsory && onDismiss && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="btn-dialog-cancel"
                  onClick={onDismiss}
                  style={{ flex: 1, padding: "10px 14px", borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm, 8px)" }}
                >
                  Skip for This Session
                </button>
              )}
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn-dialog-confirm"
                onClick={onStartDownload}
                style={{ flex: 1, padding: "10px 14px", borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm, 8px)" }}
              >
                Retry Download
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from "react";

interface UnsavedChangesModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  pageName: string;
  isSaving: boolean;
  onCancel: () => void;
  onDiscard: () => void;
  onSave: () => void;
}

export const UnsavedChangesModal: React.FC<UnsavedChangesModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  pageName,
  isSaving,
  onCancel,
  onDiscard,
  onSave,
}) => {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="modal-overlay" onClick={onCancel}>
      <div
        className="confirm-dialog-card"
        style={{ maxWidth: 440 }}
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="confirm-dialog-title">Unsaved Changes</div>
        <div
          className="confirm-dialog-body"
          style={{ color: "var(--text-secondary)", lineHeight: 1.5, margin: "12px 0 20px" }}
        >
          You have unsaved changes in <strong>{pageName}</strong>. Would you like to save your
          changes before leaving this section?
        </div>
        <div
          className="confirm-dialog-actions"
          style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", gap: 10, flexWrap: "wrap" }}
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn-dialog-cancel"
            onClick={onCancel}
            disabled={isSaving}
          >
            Cancel
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn-dialog-discard"
            onClick={onDiscard}
            disabled={isSaving}
          >
            Discard
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn-dialog-save"
            onClick={onSave}
            disabled={isSaving}
          >
            {isSaving ? "Saving..." : "Save & Continue"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE [58/62]: apps/desktop/src/renderer/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx
PURPOSE: Global React Exception Boundary
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import React, { Component, ErrorInfo, ReactNode } from "react";

interface Props {
  children: ReactNode;
  fallbackTitle?: string;
}

interface State {
  hasError: boolean;
  error: Error | null;
  errorInfo: ErrorInfo | null;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
  public state: State = {
    hasError: false,
    error: null,
    errorInfo: null,
  };

  public static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): State {
    return { hasError: true, error, errorInfo: null };
  }

  public componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: ErrorInfo) {
    console.error("[ErrorBoundary caught an uncaught exception]:", error, errorInfo);
    this.setState({ error, errorInfo });
  }

  private handleReload = () => {
    window.location.reload();
  };

  private handleReset = () => {
    this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null, errorInfo: null });
  };

  public render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div
          style={{
            minHeight: "100vh",
            display: "flex",
            flexDirection: "column",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
            padding: 32,
            backgroundColor: "var(--bg-canvas, #F0EBED)",
            color: "var(--text-primary, #16181D)",
            fontFamily: "var(--font-sans, system-ui, sans-serif)",
            textAlign: "center",
          }}
        >
          <div
            style={{
              maxWidth: 520,
              width: "100%",
              backgroundColor: "var(--bg-card, #FFFFFF)",
              border: "1px solid var(--border-subtle, #D1C8CC)",
              borderRadius: "var(--radius-lg, 2px)",
              padding: "36px 32px",
              boxShadow: "0 10px 25px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05)",
            }}
          >
            <div
              style={{
                display: "inline-flex",
                alignItems: "center",
                justifyContent: "center",
                width: 48,
                height: 48,
                borderRadius: "50%",
                backgroundColor: "#FEE2E2",
                color: "#DC2626",
                marginBottom: 20,
                fontSize: 22,
                fontWeight: "bold",
              }}
            >
              !
            </div>

            <h1
              style={{
                fontSize: 20,
                fontWeight: 700,
                marginBottom: 10,
                letterSpacing: "-0.02em",
              }}
            >
              {this.props.fallbackTitle || "Printap Exception"}
            </h1>

            <p
              style={{
                fontSize: 13,
                color: "var(--text-secondary, #424750)",
                lineHeight: 1.5,
                marginBottom: 20,
              }}
            >
              An unexpected error occurred while rendering the interface. Your print jobs and hardware
              connections remain safe in background memory.
            </p>

            {this.state.error && (
              <pre
                style={{
                  textAlign: "left",
                  fontSize: 11,
                  fontFamily: "var(--font-mono, monospace)",
                  backgroundColor: "#F9FAFB",
                  border: "1px solid var(--border-subtle, #D1C8CC)",
                  padding: 12,
                  borderRadius: "var(--radius-sm, 6px)",
                  color: "#991B1B",
                  overflowX: "auto",
                  maxHeight: 120,
                  marginBottom: 24,
                }}
              >
                {this.state.error.message}
              </pre>
            )}

            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12, justifyContent: "center" }}>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={this.handleReset}
                className="btn-secondary"
                style={{
                  padding: "10px 18px",
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  cursor: "pointer",
                }}
              >
                Attempt Recovery
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={this.handleReload}
                className="btn-primary"
                style={{
                  padding: "10px 20px",
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  cursor: "pointer",
                }}
              >
                Reload Window
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return this.props.children;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   PRINTAP SHOP AGENT — STRUCTURAL HIGH-CONTRAST DESIGN SYSTEM
   Base Background (#F0EBED) • Luminous Glow (#FFFFFF)
   Primary Text & Structure (#16181D) • Secondary Accent (#424750)
   Subtle Borders (#D1C8CC) • Sharp Wireframe Edges • Space Grotesk & JetBrains Mono
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

:root {
  /* ─── The Core Palette ─── */
  --bg-canvas: #F0EBED;                     /* Base Background: pale, washed-out pinkish-grey */
  --surface-glow: #FFFFFF;                  /* Surface / Glow: pure white luminous surface */
  --bg-card: #FFFFFF;                       /* Card backgrounds replicating luminous central glow */
  --bg-glass-card: #FFFFFF;
  --bg-glass-card-hover: #FFFFFF;
  --bg-glass-elevated: #FFFFFF;
  --bg-glass-subtle: rgba(240, 235, 237, 0.65);
  --bg-glass-input: #FFFFFF;
  --bg-glass-sidebar: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.94);

  /* ─── Primary Text & Structure (#16181D) ─── */
  --text-primary: #16181D;                  /* Deep near-black slate for headings, primary body text */
  --color-structure: #16181D;               /* Thick borders, solid buttons, dark structural pillars */
  --border-active: #16181D;

  /* ─── Secondary Accent (#424750) ─── */
  --text-secondary: #424750;                /* Medium charcoal for secondary text, metadata */
  --accent-secondary: #424750;              /* Hover states, subtle UI icons */
  --text-muted: #5e6470;                    /* Technical metadata */
  --text-faint: #888e99;

  /* ─── Subtle Borders (#D1C8CC) ─── */
  --border-subtle: #D1C8CC;                 /* Dusty mauve-grey divider lines and 1px wireframe containers */
  --border-subtle-hover: #16181D;           /* High-contrast state */
  --border-glass: #D1C8CC;                  /* 1px crisp architectural wireframe borders */

  /* ─── High Contrast Architectural Accents (Avoiding saturated colors) ─── */
  --accent-emerald: #16181D;
  --accent-emerald-light: rgba(22, 24, 29, 0.05);
  --accent-emerald-border: #D1C8CC;
  --accent-amber: #424750;
  --accent-amber-light: rgba(66, 71, 80, 0.08);
  --accent-rose: #16181D;
  --accent-rose-light: rgba(22, 24, 29, 0.06);
  --accent-indigo: #16181D;
  --accent-cyan: #16181D;
  --bg-neutral: rgba(209, 200, 204, 0.4);

  /* ─── Typography Stacks ─── */
  --font-sans: 'Space Grotesk', 'Inter', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
  --font-mono: 'JetBrains Mono', 'Geist Mono', 'Fira Code', monospace;

───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FILE [61/62]: apps/web/src/lib/customer-signaling.ts
PURPOSE: Customer WebRTC Peer: SDP Offer, Chunked File Streaming, Status Listener
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
import {
  joinSignalingChannel,
  leaveSignalingChannel,
  waitForChannelReady,
  broadcast,
  DEFAULT_ICE_SERVERS,
  waitForIceGathering,
  type SignalPayload,
  type TransferManifestOrder,
  type PrintSettings,
  type UploadedDocument,
  type QueueToken,
  type PaymentMethod,
  type PaymentStatus,
  type FinishingService,
} from "@printap/shared";
import type { RealtimeChannel } from "@supabase/supabase-js";

const CHUNK_SIZE = 64 * 1024; // 64KB chunks
const MAX_BUFFERED_AMOUNT = 1024 * 1024; // 1MB high-water mark
const LOW_WATER_MARK = 256 * 1024; // 256KB low-water mark

export interface CustomerTransferFile {
  fileId: string;
  file: File;
  pageCount: number;
  settings: PrintSettings;
}

export interface CustomerSignalingCallbacks {
  onStatusChange?: (status: string, detail?: string) => void;
  onProgress?: (pct: number, fileIndex: number, totalFiles: number) => void;
  onTokenAssigned?: (data: {
    tokenCode: string;
    printerNumber?: number;
    queuePosition?: number;
  }) => void;
  onOrderStatus?: (data: SignalPayload) => void;
  onPaymentConfirmed?: (data: SignalPayload) => void;
  onError?: (error: string) => void;
}

export type TransferStatus =
  | "connecting"
  | "connected"
  | "sending"
  | "complete"
  | "error";

export interface TransferProgress {
  status: TransferStatus;
  percent: number;
  bytesSent: number;
  totalBytes: number;
  currentFileIndex?: number;
  currentFileName?: string;
  totalFiles?: number;
  error?: string;
}

export type ProgressCallback = (progress: TransferProgress) => void;

export interface SendOrderPayload {
  shopId: string;
  sessionId: string;
  documents: UploadedDocument[];
  token: QueueToken;
  paymentMethod: PaymentMethod;
  paymentStatus: PaymentStatus;
  totalAmount: number;
  finishingServices: FinishingService[];
}

export async function computeSha256Hex(buffer: ArrayBuffer): Promise<string> {
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", buffer);
  const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
  return hashArray.map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

export async function waitForBufferDrain(dc: RTCDataChannel): Promise<void> {
  if (dc.bufferedAmount <= MAX_BUFFERED_AMOUNT) {
    return;
  }
  dc.bufferedAmountLowThreshold = LOW_WATER_MARK;
  return new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      cleanup();
      if (dc.readyState !== "open") {
        reject(new Error("DataChannel closed while draining buffer"));
      } else {
        resolve();
      }
    }, 10_000);

    const onLow = () => {
      cleanup();
      resolve();
    };

    const onClose = () => {
      cleanup();
      reject(new Error("DataChannel closed during transfer"));
    };

    const cleanup = () => {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      dc.removeEventListener("bufferedamountlow", onLow);
      dc.removeEventListener("close", onClose);
      dc.removeEventListener("error", onClose);
    };

    dc.addEventListener("bufferedamountlow", onLow);
    dc.addEventListener("close", onClose);
    dc.addEventListener("error", onClose);
  });
}

export async function streamOrderToShop(
  shopId: string,
  sessionId: string,
  manifestOrder: TransferManifestOrder,
  files: CustomerTransferFile[],
  callbacks: CustomerSignalingCallbacks
): Promise<{
  cleanup: () => void;
  channel: RealtimeChannel;
}> {
  const channel = joinSignalingChannel(shopId, true);
  const ready = await waitForChannelReady(channel, 5000);
  if (!ready) {
    throw new Error("Could not connect to shop signaling channel.");
  }

  const pc = new RTCPeerConnection({
    iceServers: DEFAULT_ICE_SERVERS,
    iceCandidatePoolSize: 10,
  });

  let isCleanedUp = false;
  const earlyCandidates: RTCIceCandidateInit[] = [];

  const cleanup = () => {
    if (isCleanedUp) return;
    isCleanedUp = true;
    try {
      pc.close();
    } catch {}
    leaveSignalingChannel(shopId).catch(() => {});
  };

  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "assign-token" },
    ({ payload }: { payload: SignalPayload }) => {
      if (payload.sessionId !== sessionId || !payload.tokenCode) return;
      callbacks.onTokenAssigned?.({
        tokenCode: payload.tokenCode,
        printerNumber: payload.printerNumber,
        queuePosition: payload.queuePosition,
      });
    }
  );

  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "order-status" },
    ({ payload }: { payload: SignalPayload }) => {
      if (payload.sessionId !== sessionId) return;
      callbacks.onOrderStatus?.(payload);
    }
  );

  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "payment-confirmed" },
    ({ payload }: { payload: SignalPayload }) => {
      if (payload.sessionId !== sessionId) return;
      callbacks.onPaymentConfirmed?.(payload);
    }
  );

  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "webrtc-ice" },
    async ({ payload }: { payload: SignalPayload }) => {
      if (payload.sessionId !== sessionId || !payload.data) return;
      const cand = payload.data as RTCIceCandidateInit;
      if (!cand.candidate) return;
      if (!pc.remoteDescription) {
        earlyCandidates.push(cand);
        return;
      }
      try {
        await pc.addIceCandidate(new RTCIceCandidate(cand));
      } catch {}
    }
  );

  channel.on(
    "broadcast",
    { event: "webrtc-answer" },
    async ({ payload }: { payload: SignalPayload }) => {
      if (payload.sessionId !== sessionId || !payload.data) return;
      if (pc.signalingState !== "have-local-offer") return;
      try {
        await pc.setRemoteDescription(
          new RTCSessionDescription(payload.data as RTCSessionDescriptionInit)
        );
        for (const cand of earlyCandidates) {
          try {
            await pc.addIceCandidate(new RTCIceCandidate(cand));
          } catch {}
        }
        earlyCandidates.length = 0;
      } catch (err) {
        callbacks.onError?.(
          err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to set remote answer"
        );
      }
    }
  );

  pc.onicecandidate = (event) => {
    if (event.candidate && event.candidate.candidate) {
      broadcast(channel, "webrtc-ice", {
        sessionId,
        data: event.candidate.toJSON(),
      });
    }
  };

  const dc = pc.createDataChannel("printap-transfer", { ordered: true });
  dc.binaryType = "arraybuffer";

  const transferPromise = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const handshakeTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
      reject(new Error("Shop terminal did not respond to P2P handshake in time."));
    }, 20_000);

    dc.onopen = async () => {
      clearTimeout(handshakeTimeout);
      try {
        callbacks.onStatusChange?.("streaming", "Streaming encrypted chunks...");

        dc.send(
          JSON.stringify({
            type: "order-manifest",
            order: manifestOrder,
          })
        );

        for (let fIdx = 0; fIdx < files.length; fIdx++) {
          const item = files[fIdx];
          const arrayBuffer = await item.file.arrayBuffer();
          const totalBytes = arrayBuffer.byteLength;
          const sha256 = await computeSha256Hex(arrayBuffer);

          dc.send(
            JSON.stringify({
              type: "file-header",
              fileId: item.fileId,
              fileName: item.file.name,
              fileSize: totalBytes,
              pageCount: item.pageCount,
              settings: item.settings,
              sha256,
            })
          );

          let offset = 0;
          while (offset < totalBytes) {
            await waitForBufferDrain(dc);
            const end = Math.min(offset + CHUNK_SIZE, totalBytes);
            dc.send(arrayBuffer.slice(offset, end));
            offset = end;

            const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((offset / totalBytes) * 100));
            callbacks.onProgress?.(pct, fIdx + 1, files.length);
          }

          await waitForBufferDrain(dc);
          dc.send(
            JSON.stringify({
              type: "file-eof",
              fileId: item.fileId,
            })
          );
        }

        await waitForBufferDrain(dc);
        dc.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "order-complete" }));
        callbacks.onStatusChange?.("transferred", "All documents beamed to counter.");
        resolve();
      } catch (err) {
        reject(err);
      }
    };

    dc.onerror = () => {
      clearTimeout(handshakeTimeout);
      reject(new Error("WebRTC DataChannel transfer interrupted."));
    };
  });

  const offer = await pc.createOffer();
  await pc.setLocalDescription(offer);
  await waitForIceGathering(pc, 800);

  const localOffer = pc.localDescription || offer;
  await broadcast(channel, "webrtc-offer", {
    sessionId,
    data: { type: localOffer.type, sdp: localOffer.sdp },
  });

  await transferPromise;

  return { cleanup, channel };
}

/**
 * Compatible wrapper around streamOrderToShop matching previous UI callbacks.
 */
export async function sendOrderToShop(
  payload: SendOrderPayload,
  _printTicket: string,
  onProgress: ProgressCallback
): Promise<void> {
  const {
    shopId,
    sessionId,
    documents,
    token,
    paymentMethod,
    paymentStatus,
    totalAmount,
    finishingServices,
  } = payload;

  const totalBytes = documents.reduce((acc, d) => acc + d.file.size, 0);

  onProgress({
    status: "connecting",
    percent: 0,
    bytesSent: 0,
    totalBytes,
    totalFiles: documents.length,
    currentFileIndex: 1,
    currentFileName: documents[0]?.file.name,
  });

  const manifestOrder: TransferManifestOrder = {
    tokenCode: token.tokenCode,
    pickupPin: token.pickupPin,
    queuePosition: token.queuePosition,
    totalAmount,
    paymentMethod,
    paymentStatus,
    finishingServices: finishingServices?.map((f) => ({
      type: f.type,
      label: f.label,
      price: f.price,
    })),
    totalFiles: documents.length,
  };

  const files: CustomerTransferFile[] = documents.map((d) => ({
    fileId: d.id,
    file: d.file,
    pageCount: d.pageCount,
    settings: d.settings,
  }));

  try {
    await streamOrderToShop(
      shopId,
      sessionId,
      manifestOrder,
      files,
      {
        onStatusChange: (status) => {
          if (status === "streaming") {
            onProgress({
              status: "sending",
              percent: 0,
              bytesSent: 0,
              totalBytes,
              totalFiles: documents.length,
              currentFileIndex: 1,
              currentFileName: documents[0]?.file.name,
            });
          }
        },
        onProgress: (pct, fileIndex, totalFiles) => {
          onProgress({
            status: "sending",
            percent: pct,
            bytesSent: Math.round((pct / 100) * totalBytes),
            totalBytes,
            currentFileIndex: fileIndex,
            currentFileName: documents[fileIndex - 1]?.file.name,
            totalFiles,
          });
        },
        onError: (err) => {
          onProgress({
            status: "error",
            percent: 0,
            bytesSent: 0,
            totalBytes,
            error: err,
          });
        },
      }
    );

    onProgress({
      status: "complete",
      percent: 100,
      bytesSent: totalBytes,
      totalBytes,
      currentFileIndex: documents.length,
      currentFileName: documents[documents.length - 1]?.file.name,
      totalFiles: documents.length,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
    onProgress({
      status: "error",
      percent: 0,
      bytesSent: 0,
      totalBytes,
      error: msg,
    });
    throw err;
  }
}

/**
 * Request an authoritative, collision-free local token directly from the Shop Desktop App.
 */
export async function requestTokenFromShop(
  shopId: string,
  params: {
    sessionId: string;
    hasColor: boolean;
    totalAmount?: number;
    fileCount?: number;
  },
  timeoutMs: number = 3000
): Promise<QueueToken> {
  const channel = joinSignalingChannel(shopId);
  await waitForChannelReady(channel, 1200);

  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    let settled = false;
    let retryInterval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
    let timeoutTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

    const cleanup = () => {
      if (retryInterval) clearInterval(retryInterval);
      if (timeoutTimer) clearTimeout(timeoutTimer);
    };

    const onTokenAssigned = ({ payload }: { payload: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
      const p = payload as unknown as SignalPayload;
      if (p.sessionId === params.sessionId && p.tokenCode) {
        if (!settled) {
          settled = true;
          cleanup();
          resolve({
            tokenCode: p.tokenCode,
            queuePosition: p.queuePosition || 1,
            estimatedWaitMinutes: (p.queuePosition || 1) * 2,
          });
        }
      }
    };

    channel.on("broadcast", { event: "assign-token" }, onTokenAssigned);
    channel.on("broadcast", { event: "order-status" }, onTokenAssigned);

    const transmit = () => {
      if (!settled) {
        broadcast(channel, "request-token", {
          sessionId: params.sessionId,
          hasColor: params.hasColor,
          totalAmount: params.totalAmount,
          fileCount: params.fileCount,
        });
      }
    };

    transmit();
    retryInterval = setInterval(transmit, 250);

    timeoutTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (!settled) {
        settled = true;
        cleanup();
        const suffix = params.sessionId ? params.sessionId.slice(-3).toUpperCase() : Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5).toUpperCase();
        const prefix = params.hasColor ? "C" : "B";
        resolve({
          tokenCode: `#${prefix}-${suffix}`,
          queuePosition: 1,
          estimatedWaitMinutes: 2,
        });
      }
    }, timeoutMs);
  });
}

import { describe, it } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {
  calculateSheets,
  calculateOrderSheets,
  calculateDocumentPrice,
  calculateFinishingCost,
  calculateOrderTotal,
  parsePageRangeCount,
  isValidEmail,
  isValidSubdomainLabel,
  isReservedSubdomain,
  SESSION_STORAGE_KEY,
  isOrderCompleted,
  isOrderFailed,
  type PrintSettings,
  type ShopRateCard,
  type FinishingService,
  type PersistedSession,
  type QueueToken,
} from "../packages/shared/src/index";

// -------------------------------------------------------------
// Helper: SHA-256 calculation matching customer-signaling & shop-signaling
// -------------------------------------------------------------
async function computeSha256Hex(buffer: ArrayBuffer): Promise<string> {
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", buffer);
  const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
  return hashArray.map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------
// Helper: Image MIME detection matching Electron Main detectImageMime
// -------------------------------------------------------------
function detectImageMime(uint8: Uint8Array): string {
  if (uint8[0] === 0x89 && uint8[1] === 0x50 && uint8[2] === 0x4e && uint8[3] === 0x47)
    return "image/png";
  if (uint8[0] === 0xff && uint8[1] === 0xd8 && uint8[2] === 0xff)
    return "image/jpeg";
  if (uint8[0] === 0x47 && uint8[1] === 0x49 && uint8[2] === 0x46)
    return "image/gif";
  if (uint8[0] === 0x42 && uint8[1] === 0x4d) return "image/bmp";
  if (uint8[0] === 0x52 && uint8[1] === 0x49 && uint8[2] === 0x46 && uint8[3] === 0x46)
    return "image/webp";
  return "image/png";
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------
// Helper: Spooler options mapping matching apps/desktop/src/main/index.ts
// -------------------------------------------------------------
interface PrintPdfOptions {
  copies?: number;
  colorMode?: "bw" | "color";
  duplex?: "single" | "double";
  scale?: "fit" | "noscale";
  printer?: string;
  orientation?: "portrait" | "landscape";
}

function mapToPtpOptions(options?: PrintPdfOptions) {
  const copyCount = Math.max(1, Math.min(500, options?.copies ?? 1));
  const printOptions: {
    scale: string;
    copies: number;
    monochrome: boolean;
    side: "duplex" | "simplex";
    printer?: string;
    orientation?: "portrait" | "landscape";
  } = {
    scale: options?.scale || "noscale",
    copies: copyCount,
    monochrome: options?.colorMode === "bw",
    side: options?.duplex === "double" ? "duplex" : "simplex",
  };

  if (options?.printer) {
    printOptions.printer = options.printer;
  }
  if (options?.orientation) {
    printOptions.orientation = options.orientation;
  }
  return printOptions;
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------
// Helper: PrintHistory revenue stats matching apps/desktop/src/renderer/components/PrintHistory.tsx
// -------------------------------------------------------------
interface OrderRecord {
  id: string;
  status: string;
  payment_method: string;
  total_amount: number | string;
}

function computePrintHistoryStats(orders: OrderRecord[]) {
  let totalRevenue = 0;
  let upiRevenue = 0;
  let cashRevenue = 0;
  let settledCount = 0;

  orders.forEach((o) => {
    if (o.status === "cancelled" || o.status === "failed" || o.status === "failed_refunded") {
      return;
    }
    settledCount++;
    const amt = Number(o.total_amount) || 0;
    totalRevenue += amt;
    if (o.payment_method === "upi") upiRevenue += amt;
    else if (o.payment_method === "cash") cashRevenue += amt;
  });

  return {
    count: settledCount,
    totalRevenue,
    upiRevenue,
    cashRevenue,
  };
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------
// Helper: 30-min Pending Queue Sweeper matching App.tsx
// -------------------------------------------------------------
interface QueueJob {
  sessionId: string;
  enqueuedAt: string;
  files: { buffer: ArrayBuffer }[];
}

function sweepExpiredJobs(queue: QueueJob[], nowMs: number, expiryMs = 30 * 60 * 1000) {
  const expired: QueueJob[] = [];
  const active: QueueJob[] = [];

  queue.forEach((job) => {
    const age = nowMs - new Date(job.enqueuedAt).getTime();
    if (age > expiryMs) {
      // Memory zeroing
      job.files.forEach((f) => {
        f.buffer = new ArrayBuffer(0);
      });
      expired.push(job);
    } else {
      active.push(job);
    }
  });

  return { active, expired };
}

// =============================================================
// TEST SUITES
// =============================================================

describe("1. End-to-End SHA-256 WebRTC File Integrity", () => {
  it("should generate deterministic SHA-256 hex digest for an ArrayBuffer", async () => {
    const text = "Enterprise reliability verification for Printap PDF transfer";
    const enc = new TextEncoder();
    const buf = enc.encode(text).buffer;

    const hash = await computeSha256Hex(buf);
    assert.strictEqual(typeof hash, "string");
    assert.strictEqual(hash.length, 64);

    // Same buffer produces identical hash
    const hash2 = await computeSha256Hex(buf);
    assert.strictEqual(hash, hash2);
  });

  it("should detect corrupted data and reject mismatched SHA-256", async () => {
    const originalBuf = new Uint8Array([1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8]).buffer;
    const corruptedBuf = new Uint8Array([1, 2, 3, 4, 99, 6, 7, 8]).buffer;

    const expectedHash = await computeSha256Hex(originalBuf);
    const actualHash = await computeSha256Hex(corruptedBuf);

    assert.notStrictEqual(expectedHash, actualHash);
  });
});

describe("2. Image MIME Magic-Byte Detection (Step 3 Fix)", () => {
  it("should correctly identify PNG using 0x89 0x50 0x4e 0x47", () => {
    const pngHeader = new Uint8Array([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a]);
    assert.strictEqual(detectImageMime(pngHeader), "image/png");
  });

  it("should correctly identify JPEG using 0xff 0xd8 0xff", () => {
    const jpegHeader = new Uint8Array([0xff, 0xd8, 0xff, 0xe0, 0x00, 0x10]);
    assert.strictEqual(detectImageMime(jpegHeader), "image/jpeg");
  });

  it("should correctly identify GIF using 0x47 0x49 0x46", () => {
    const gifHeader = new Uint8Array([0x47, 0x49, 0x46, 0x38, 0x39, 0x61]);
    assert.strictEqual(detectImageMime(gifHeader), "image/gif");
  });

  it("should correctly identify BMP using 0x42 0x4d", () => {
    const bmpHeader = new Uint8Array([0x42, 0x4d, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00]);
    assert.strictEqual(detectImageMime(bmpHeader), "image/bmp");
  });

  it("should correctly identify WebP using 0x52 0x49 0x46 0x46", () => {
    const webpHeader = new Uint8Array([0x52, 0x49, 0x46, 0x46, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00]);
    assert.strictEqual(detectImageMime(webpHeader), "image/webp");
  });

  it("should fallback to image/png for unrecognized formats", () => {
    const unknown = new Uint8Array([0x00, 0x01, 0x02, 0x03]);
    assert.strictEqual(detectImageMime(unknown), "image/png");
  });
});

describe("3. Native Duplex, Monochrome & Copies Print Spooler Translation (Steps 3 & 4)", () => {
  it("should map double-sided to duplex and single-sided to simplex", () => {
    const doubleRes = mapToPtpOptions({ duplex: "double" });
    assert.strictEqual(doubleRes.side, "duplex");

    const singleRes = mapToPtpOptions({ duplex: "single" });
    assert.strictEqual(singleRes.side, "simplex");
  });

  it("should map colorMode bw to monochrome true and color to monochrome false", () => {
    const bwRes = mapToPtpOptions({ colorMode: "bw" });
    assert.strictEqual(bwRes.monochrome, true);

    const colorRes = mapToPtpOptions({ colorMode: "color" });
    assert.strictEqual(colorRes.monochrome, false);
  });

  it("should pass native copies with clamp between 1 and 500", () => {
    const normal = mapToPtpOptions({ copies: 3 });
    assert.strictEqual(normal.copies, 3);

    const zero = mapToPtpOptions({ copies: 0 });
    assert.strictEqual(zero.copies, 1);

    const huge = mapToPtpOptions({ copies: 1000 });
    assert.strictEqual(huge.copies, 500);
  });
});

describe("4. Print History Revenue & Cancellation Math (Step 7)", () => {
  it("should strictly exclude cancelled and failed orders from revenue totals", () => {
    const orders: OrderRecord[] = [
      { id: "1", status: "completed", payment_method: "upi", total_amount: 150 },
      { id: "2", status: "completed", payment_method: "cash", total_amount: 50 },
      { id: "3", status: "cancelled", payment_method: "upi", total_amount: 500 },
      { id: "4", status: "failed", payment_method: "cash", total_amount: 200 },
      { id: "5", status: "failed_refunded", payment_method: "upi", total_amount: 300 },
    ];

    const stats = computePrintHistoryStats(orders);
    assert.strictEqual(stats.count, 2);
    assert.strictEqual(stats.totalRevenue, 200);
    assert.strictEqual(stats.upiRevenue, 150);
    assert.strictEqual(stats.cashRevenue, 50);
  });
});

describe("5. 30-Minute Pending Queue Sweeper & Heap Memory Deallocation (Step 6)", () => {
  it("should sweep jobs older than 30 minutes and zero their ArrayBuffers", () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    const oldTime = new Date(now - 31 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(); // 31 mins ago
    const freshTime = new Date(now - 5 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(); // 5 mins ago

    const dummyBuf1 = new ArrayBuffer(1024 * 1024); // 1 MB
    const dummyBuf2 = new ArrayBuffer(2 * 1024 * 1024); // 2 MB

    const queue: QueueJob[] = [
      {
        sessionId: "sess-expired-1",
        enqueuedAt: oldTime,
        files: [{ buffer: dummyBuf1 }],
      },
      {
        sessionId: "sess-active-2",
        enqueuedAt: freshTime,
        files: [{ buffer: dummyBuf2 }],
      },
    ];

    const { active, expired } = sweepExpiredJobs(queue, now);

    assert.strictEqual(expired.length, 1);
    assert.strictEqual(expired[0].sessionId, "sess-expired-1");
    assert.strictEqual(expired[0].files[0].buffer.byteLength, 0, "Buffer should be deallocated to 0 bytes");

    assert.strictEqual(active.length, 1);
    assert.strictEqual(active[0].sessionId, "sess-active-2");
    assert.strictEqual(active[0].files[0].buffer.byteLength, 2 * 1024 * 1024, "Active buffer remains intact");
  });
});

describe("6. Disconnected Printers Pruning Logic (Step 8)", () => {
  it("should filter and identify stale printer records to delete", () => {
    const savedAssignments = {
      "HP LaserJet Pro": { paper_size: "A4", color_capable: false },
      "Epson L3150": { paper_size: "A4", color_capable: true },
      "Old Disconnected Canon": { paper_size: "A3", color_capable: true },
    };

    const physicalPrinters = ["HP LaserJet Pro", "Epson L3150"];
    const activeNames = Object.keys(savedAssignments).filter((name) =>
      physicalPrinters.includes(name)
    );

    assert.deepStrictEqual(activeNames, ["HP LaserJet Pro", "Epson L3150"]);
    assert.ok(!activeNames.includes("Old Disconnected Canon"));
  });
});

describe("7. Pricing Engine & Rates Verification", () => {
  const rateCard: ShopRateCard = {
    priceBwPerPage: 2.0,
    priceColorPerPage: 10.0,
    finishingServices: [],
  };

  it("should accurately calculate single-sided sheets and price", () => {
    const settings: PrintSettings = {
      copies: 1,
      colorMode: "bw",
      duplex: "single",
      pagesPerSheet: 1,
      pageFit: "fit",
      orientation: "portrait",
    };

    const doc = {
      id: "doc-1",
      type: "pdf" as const,
      file: {} as File,
      pageCount: 10,
      settings,
    };

    const sheets = calculateSheets(10, settings);
    assert.strictEqual(sheets, 10);

    const price = calculateDocumentPrice(doc, rateCard);
    assert.strictEqual(price, 20.0); // 10 sheets * 2.0 * 1 copy
  });

  it("should accurately calculate double-sided duplex sheets and price", () => {
    const settings: PrintSettings = {
      copies: 1,
      colorMode: "bw",
      duplex: "double",
      pagesPerSheet: 1,
      pageFit: "fit",
      orientation: "portrait",
    };

    const doc = {
      id: "doc-2",
      type: "pdf" as const,
      file: {} as File,
      pageCount: 10,
      settings,
    };

    const sheets = calculateSheets(10, settings);
    assert.strictEqual(sheets, 5); // 10 pages duplex = 5 sheets

    const price = calculateDocumentPrice(doc, rateCard);
    assert.strictEqual(price, 10.0); // 5 sheets * 2.0 * 1 copy
  });

  it("should accurately calculate 2-pages-per-sheet duplex", () => {
    const settings: PrintSettings = {
      copies: 2,
      colorMode: "bw",
      duplex: "double",
      pagesPerSheet: 2,
      pageFit: "fit",
      orientation: "portrait",
    };

    const doc = {
      id: "doc-3",
      type: "pdf" as const,
      file: {} as File,
      pageCount: 8,
      settings,
    };

    // 8 document pages with 2 pages/sheet = 4 logical pages -> duplex = 2 physical sheets
    const sheets = calculateSheets(8, settings);
    assert.strictEqual(sheets, 2);

    const price = calculateDocumentPrice(doc, rateCard);
    // 2 physical sheets * 2.0 per sheet * 2 copies = 8.0
    assert.strictEqual(price, 8.0);
  });

  it("should compute finishing services cost accurately", () => {
    const settings: PrintSettings = {
      copies: 2,
      colorMode: "bw",
      duplex: "single",
      pagesPerSheet: 1,
      pageFit: "fit",
      orientation: "portrait",
    };

    const doc = {
      id: "doc-4",
      type: "pdf" as const,
      file: {} as File,
      pageCount: 5,
      settings,
    };

    const finishingServices: FinishingService[] = [
      {
        type: "corner_staple",
        label: "Corner Staple",
        price: 5.0,
        chargeUnit: "per_copy",
        enabled: true,
      },
      {
        type: "lamination",
        label: "Lamination",
        price: 10.0,
        chargeUnit: "per_sheet",
        enabled: true,
      },
    ];

    // Staple: per_copy -> 5.0 * 2 copies = 10.0
    const stapleCost = calculateFinishingCost(doc, [finishingServices[0]]);
    assert.strictEqual(stapleCost, 10.0);

    // Lamination: per_sheet -> 10.0 * 5 sheets * 2 copies = 100.0
    const laminationCost = calculateFinishingCost(doc, [finishingServices[1]]);
    assert.strictEqual(laminationCost, 100.0);
  });

  it("should accurately calculate color double-sided duplex sheets and price without blocking duplex", () => {
    const settings: PrintSettings = {
      copies: 1,
      colorMode: "color",
      duplex: "double",
      pagesPerSheet: 1,
      pageFit: "fit",
      orientation: "portrait",
    };

    const doc = {
      id: "doc-color-duplex",
      type: "pdf" as const,
      file: {} as File,
      pageCount: 10,
      settings,
    };

    // 10 color pages in duplex should be 5 physical sheets
    const sheets = calculateSheets(10, settings);
    assert.strictEqual(sheets, 5);

    // 5 sheets * 10.0 (color rate) * 1 copy = 50.0
    const price = calculateDocumentPrice(doc, rateCard);
    assert.strictEqual(price, 50.0);
  });

  it("should accurately parse page range and calculate sheets", () => {
    assert.strictEqual(parsePageRangeCount("1-5, 8, 10-12", 20), 9); // 1,2,3,4,5,8,10,11,12 -> 9 pages
    assert.strictEqual(parsePageRangeCount("1-5", 5), 5);
    assert.strictEqual(parsePageRangeCount("3", 10), 1);
    assert.strictEqual(parsePageRangeCount("", 15), 15);

    const settings: PrintSettings = {
      copies: 1,
      colorMode: "bw",
      duplex: "single",
      pagesPerSheet: 1,
      pageFit: "fit",
      orientation: "portrait",
      pageRange: "1-4", // 4 pages out of 20
    };

    const sheets = calculateSheets(20, settings);
    assert.strictEqual(sheets, 4);
  });

  it("should accurately price image batch with mixed color modes and copies", () => {
    const docs = [
      {
        id: "img-1",
        type: "image" as const,
        file: {} as File,
        pageCount: 1,
        settings: {
          copies: 1,
          colorMode: "bw" as const,
          duplex: "single" as const,
          pagesPerSheet: 2 as const,
          pageFit: "fit" as const,
          orientation: "portrait" as const,
        },
      },
      {
        id: "img-2",
        type: "image" as const,
        file: {} as File,
        pageCount: 1,
        settings: {
          copies: 2,
          colorMode: "color" as const, // Mixed color mode in batch
          duplex: "single" as const,
          pagesPerSheet: 2 as const,
          pageFit: "fit" as const,
          orientation: "portrait" as const,
        },
      },
    ];

    // 2 images with 2-in-1 = 1 logical page = 1 sheet per copy. Max copies = 2 -> totalSheets = 2.
    // Batch contains a color image, so color rate (10.0) applies -> 2 sheets * 10.0 = 20.0
    const totalSheets = calculateOrderSheets(docs);
    assert.strictEqual(totalSheets, 2);

    const total = calculateOrderTotal(docs, rateCard, []);
    assert.strictEqual(total, 20.0);
  });
});

    assert.strictEqual(isValidEmail("owner.shop+tag@mellod.in"), true);
    assert.strictEqual(isValidEmail("invalid-email"), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isValidEmail("@domain.com"), false);
  });

  it("should protect reserved subdomains and validate shop slugs", () => {
    assert.strictEqual(isReservedSubdomain("admin"), true);
    assert.strictEqual(isReservedSubdomain("api"), true);
    assert.strictEqual(isReservedSubdomain("platform"), true);
    assert.strictEqual(isReservedSubdomain("dashboard"), true);
    assert.strictEqual(isReservedSubdomain("central-xerox"), false);

    assert.strictEqual(isValidSubdomainLabel("shop-123"), true);
    assert.strictEqual(isValidSubdomainLabel("-invalid"), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isValidSubdomainLabel("invalid-"), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isValidSubdomainLabel("UPPERCASE"), false);
  });
});

describe("9. 10-Minute Onboarding Login Retention Policy", () => {
  const LOGIN_SESSION_MAX_AGE_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;

  function formatTime(ms: number): string {
    const totalSeconds = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 1000));
    const minutes = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);
    const seconds = totalSeconds % 60;
    return `${minutes}m ${seconds.toString().padStart(2, "0")}s`;
  }

  function checkSessionExpired(loginAt: number, now: number): boolean {
    return now - loginAt >= LOGIN_SESSION_MAX_AGE_MS;
  }

  it("should enforce exactly 10 minutes TTL (600,000 ms)", () => {
    assert.strictEqual(LOGIN_SESSION_MAX_AGE_MS, 600_000);
  });

  it("should format remaining session time into human readable Xm Ys", () => {
    assert.strictEqual(formatTime(600_000), "10m 00s");
    assert.strictEqual(formatTime(585_000), "9m 45s");
    assert.strictEqual(formatTime(65_000), "1m 05s");
    assert.strictEqual(formatTime(5_000), "0m 05s");
    assert.strictEqual(formatTime(0), "0m 00s");
  });

  it("should treat sessions younger than 10 minutes as active", () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    const loginAt = now - 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5 minutes ago
    assert.strictEqual(checkSessionExpired(loginAt, now), false);

    const loginJustNow = now - 1000; // 1 second ago
    assert.strictEqual(checkSessionExpired(loginJustNow, now), false);

    const loginAt9m59s = now - (9 * 60 + 59) * 1000; // 9m 59s ago
    assert.strictEqual(checkSessionExpired(loginAt9m59s, now), false);
  });

  it("should expire sessions at or exceeding 10 minutes", () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    const loginAt10m = now - 10 * 60 * 1000; // exactly 10 minutes ago
    assert.strictEqual(checkSessionExpired(loginAt10m, now), true);

    const loginAt15m = now - 15 * 60 * 1000; // 15 minutes ago
    assert.strictEqual(checkSessionExpired(loginAt15m, now), true);
  });
});

describe("10. User Side Session Retention & Lifecycle Termination", () => {
  const shopId = "test-shop-uuid-123";
  const storageKey = SESSION_STORAGE_KEY(shopId);

  // Mock localStorage for lifecycle testing
  class MockLocalStorage {
    private store: Record<string, string> = {};
    getItem(key: string): string | null {
      return this.store[key] ?? null;
    }
    setItem(key: string, value: string): void {
      this.store[key] = value;
    }
    removeItem(key: string): void {
      delete this.store[key];
    }
    clear(): void {
      this.store = {};
    }
  }

  it("should generate the deterministic customer session storage key for a shop", () => {
    assert.strictEqual(storageKey, "printap_active_order_test-shop-uuid-123");
    assert.strictEqual(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY("central-xerox"), "printap_active_order_central-xerox");
  });

  it("should accurately identify completed order states", () => {
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderCompleted("picked_up"), true);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderCompleted("completed"), true);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderCompleted("queued"), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderCompleted("printing"), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderCompleted("ready_for_pickup"), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderCompleted("pending_payment"), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderCompleted("cancelled"), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderCompleted("failed"), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderCompleted(undefined), false);
  });

  it("should accurately identify failed order states", () => {
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderFailed("cancelled"), true);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderFailed("failed"), true);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderFailed("failed_refunded"), true);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderFailed("error"), true);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderFailed("picked_up"), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderFailed("completed"), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderFailed("queued"), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderFailed("printing"), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderFailed("ready_for_pickup"), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderFailed(undefined), false);
  });

  it("should retain active order session across serialization and restoration", () => {
    const storage = new MockLocalStorage();
    const token: QueueToken = {
      tokenCode: "#105",
      queuePosition: 1,
      estimatedWaitMinutes: 3,
    };

    const session: PersistedSession = {
      orderId: "session-order-uuid-abc",
      shopId,
      token,
      status: "printing",
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      totalAmount: 25.5,
      documentCount: 2,
      printTicket: "ticket-12345",
      isPaymentConfirmed: false,
    };

    // Customer places order -> saved to storage
    storage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(session));

    // Customer refreshes page -> restored from storage
    const raw = storage.getItem(storageKey);
    assert.notStrictEqual(raw, null);
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw!) as PersistedSession;

    assert.strictEqual(parsed.orderId, "session-order-uuid-abc");
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.token.tokenCode, "#105");
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.status, "printing");
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.totalAmount, 25.5);
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.documentCount, 2);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderCompleted(parsed.status), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderFailed(parsed.status), false);
  });

  it("should retain session when order is ready for pickup or awaiting payment", () => {
    const storage = new MockLocalStorage();
    const token: QueueToken = {
      tokenCode: "#105",
      queuePosition: 1,
      estimatedWaitMinutes: 0,
    };

    const session: PersistedSession = {
      orderId: "session-order-uuid-abc",
      shopId,
      token,
      status: "ready_for_pickup",
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      totalAmount: 18.0,
      documentCount: 1,
      isPaymentConfirmed: true,
    };

    storage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(session));

    const raw = storage.getItem(storageKey);
    assert.notStrictEqual(raw, null);
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw!) as PersistedSession;
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.status, "ready_for_pickup");
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.isPaymentConfirmed, true);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderCompleted(parsed.status), false);
    assert.strictEqual(isOrderFailed(parsed.status), false);
  });

  it("should end (purge) session when order is completed or picked up", () => {
    const storage = new MockLocalStorage();
    const token: QueueToken = {
      tokenCode: "#105",
      queuePosition: 1,
      estimatedWaitMinutes: 0,
    };

    storage.setItem(
      storageKey,
      JSON.stringify({
        orderId: "session-order-uuid-abc",
        shopId,
        token,
        status: "printing",
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      })
    );

    // Order completes at counter -> status updated to picked_up
    const newStatus = "picked_up";
    if (isOrderCompleted(newStatus) || isOrderFailed(newStatus)) {
      storage.removeItem(storageKey);
    }

    assert.strictEqual(storage.getItem(storageKey), null);
  });

  it("should end (purge) session when order fails, is cancelled, or errors", () => {
    const failedStatuses = ["cancelled", "failed", "failed_refunded", "error"];

    for (const failure of failedStatuses) {
      const storage = new MockLocalStorage();
      storage.setItem(
        storageKey,
        JSON.stringify({
          orderId: "session-order-uuid-abc",
          shopId,
          token: { tokenCode: "#105", queuePosition: 1, estimatedWaitMinutes: 0 },
          status: "queued",
          createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        })
      );

      // Failure occurs
      if (isOrderCompleted(failure) || isOrderFailed(failure)) {
        storage.removeItem(storageKey);
      }

      assert.strictEqual(
        storage.getItem(storageKey),
        null,
        `Expected session to be purged on status ${failure}`
      );
    }
  });
});
